### [1] [Light meson decays at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24472)
*Kailu Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了BESIII实验利用世界上最大的J/ψ事例样本，通过辐射跃迁J/ψ → γη/γη′研究η和η′介子物理的若干最新进展，涵盖轻介子谱学中的反常结构、多体强子衰变机制、电磁跃迁形状因子测量及稀有轻子末态衰变搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用BESIII积累的迄今最大统计量J/ψ事例样本，系统研究η和η′介子的产生与衰变特性，以检验QCD手征对称性破缺、胶球混杂态贡献、U_A(1)反常及轻强子动力学等基本问题。

Method: 基于J/ψ → γη/γη′过程产生的高纯度η/η′事例，结合BESIII高精度电磁量能器与带电粒子径迹重建能力，采用事件筛选、不变质量谱分析、角分布拟合、形式因子参数化及背景抑制等方法，开展多通道衰变分支比、运动学分布与新物理信号搜寻。

Result: 观测到η′ → π⁰π⁰η衰变中π⁰π⁰阈值附近的尖锐cusp结构；精确测定η → 3π与η′ → 4π衰变的CP破坏敏感观测量及动力学相位；首次在η/η′ → γe⁺e⁻与η′ → π⁺π⁻l⁺l⁻过程中测得类时电磁跃迁形状因子；并对η → l⁺l⁻及η/η′ → l⁺l⁻l⁺l⁻（l = e, μ）等超弱衰变给出迄今最严格上限。

Conclusion: BESIII在η/η′物理领域实现了从精度测量到新现象探索的系统性突破，为理解非微扰QCD中手征动力学、矢量介子主导（VMD）失效区域及可能的新物理耦合提供了关键实验证据。

Abstract: The world's largest sample of $J/\psi$ events accumulated with the BESIII detector offers a unique opportunity to investigate $\eta$ and $\eta'$ physics via the radiative transition processes $J/\psi \to \gamma\eta/\eta'$. A selection of recent highlights in light meson spectroscopy achieved at BESIII is reviewed in these proceedings, including evidence for the cusp effect in $\eta' \to \pi^0\pi^0\eta$, precision studies of the decay mechanisms of $\eta \to 3\pi$ and $\eta' \to 4\pi$, electromagnetic transition form factor measurements with $\eta/\eta' \to \gamma e^+e^-$ and $\eta' \to \pi^+\pi^-l^+l^-$, as well as searches for rare decays of $\eta$ and $\eta'$ into lepton pairs, including $\eta \to l^+l^-$ and $\eta/\eta' \to l^+l^-l^+l^-$~($l = e, \mu$).

</details>


### [2] [ceci n'est pas un tagger, can an LLM flavour-tag a jet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24954)
*Andrea Coccaro,Andrea Milici,Lucio Derin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型（LLM）在高能物理中是否能仅凭预训练知识和喷注重建组分，直接完成强子喷注味分类任务（b/c/light/τ），不依赖代码编写或传统算法实现；实验表明LLM具备非平凡的味区分能力，并能生成符合物理直觉、与真实味标签相关的自然语言推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验LLM预训练所获科学知识是否足以支撑高能物理中的定量推断任务，特别是脱离传统算法框架、仅基于文本化输入与内在知识进行喷注味识别。

Method: 使用公开ATLAS味标记数据集，将喷注的重建组分（如粒子类型、动量等）以结构化文本形式输入LLM；约束模型不得生成代码或调用外部算法，仅通过其内部参数化知识进行端到端味概率预测（b/c/light/τ）并输出自然语言推理。

Result: 1) LLM在无微调、无代码生成条件下实现显著高于随机的喷注味分类性能，与近期ATLAS专用味标定算法具有可比性；2) 模型生成的自然语言理由中提及的物理概念（如顶夸克衰变、D介子寿命、软轻子等）与真实喷注味高度相关，复现了标准味鉴别特征。

Conclusion: LLM不仅可作为科学知识的查询接口，更具备执行特定物理推理任务的潜力；喷注味分类构成一个可控‘物理推理实验室’，为探索如何将数十年积累的科学语料转化为可泛化的任务上下文提供了新范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of reasoning about scientific problems, effectively mastering and synthesizing entire fields of research and providing, for the first time, access to a distilled and queryable form of scientific knowledge. As the integration of LLMs rapidly spreads across the large collaborations of high-energy physics, aiming to re-think and optimize analysis workflows, it remains unclear whether the knowledge acquired during pretraining can support quantitative inference on physical data. Moving beyond the applications currently pursued by the community, we pose a simple question: can an LLM classify the flavour of a hadronic jet? An LLM is therefore asked to assign flavour probabilities (b, c, light, $\tau$) to jets from a public ATLAS flavour-tagging sample, using only reconstructed constituents. Under a small set of constraints, the model is not allowed to write code or implement conventional algorithms and must instead perform the inference directly from its pretrained knowledge and the available inputs. We report two complementary results and make publicly available, through a GitHub repository, all the code required to reproduce the experiment and obtain the quantitative results presented in this work. First, the model achieves non-trivial discrimination between flavours, which we compare with recent ATLAS flavour-tagging algorithms. Second, the model accompanies each prediction with a natural language rationale. The physical concepts invoked in these rationales correlate with the truth flavour and reproduce the features traditionally associated with each jet category. Flavour tagging thus provides a laboratory for broader questions: how can we actively leverage the corpus of scientific knowledge accumulated over decades of research? Could LLMs serve as a general source of scientific context for task-specific algorithms?

</details>


### [3] [Search for new physics with charm data at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25753)
*Xinyu Chai*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了BESIII实验在J/ψ和ψ(3686)共振峰上利用世界最大数据样本，开展超越标准模型（BSM）物理的若干前沿探针：轻子数/重子数破坏（LNV/BNV）、带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）及类粲偶素弱衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型在解释宇宙正反物质不对称、中微子质量、振荡与能谱层级结构等方面存在根本性困难，暴露出其自然性与稳定性问题，亟需新物理机制补充。

Method: 基于BESIII实验在J/ψ和ψ(3686)共振能区采集的迄今全球最大统计量数据样本，系统开展LNV/BNV、CLFV及charmonium弱衰变过程的高精度唯象分析与实验搜寻。

Result: 汇总了BESIII合作组近期在上述三类BSM敏感过程中的实验结果，包括各通道的上限设定、显著性检验与模型约束。

Conclusion: BESIII在强子对撞机不可达的能量标下，以高亮度、低本底、高分辨率优势，为探索轻子味结构、重子数守恒破缺及夸克—轻子统一等深层问题提供了独特且互补的实验窗口。

Abstract: Up to now, the Standard Model (SM) remains confronted with several outstanding issues, including the matter-antimatter asymmetry in the universe, as well as the masses, oscillations, and hierarchical structure of neutrinos. These challenges are closely tied to the naturalness and stability of the SM, and have motivated numerous proposals for new physics beyond the SM. The BESIII experiment has accumulated the world's largest data samples at the $J/\psi$ and $\psi(3686)$ resonances, providing an ideal environment for probing signatures of physics beyond the SM. This proceeding presents a summary of recent results published by the BESIII Collaboration on the searches for lepton/baryon number violation (LNV/BNV), charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV), and charmonium weak decays.

</details>


### [4] [Low-Latency Beam-Background Suppression for the BESIII Trigger System Using Machine Learning Trained on Experimental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26148)
*Hanlin Zhang,Minggang Zhao,Junhao Yin,Boping Chen,Jingzhou Zhao,Wenxuan Gong,Ke Li*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实验数据驱动的机器学习方法，用于在BESIII实验中实现触发级束流本底抑制，通过紧凑型神经网络分类器显著降低假阳性率，并具备FPGA低延迟硬件实现潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高亮度正负电子对撞机中束流诱导本底会抬升触发与数据获取率，其依赖于束流与机器参数，需结合数据驱动与模拟研究。

Method: 使用BESIII实验数据训练紧凑型神经网络分类器，分别基于仅径迹+飞行时间信息和仅电磁量能器信息两种触发配置，各使用48个二元一级触发条件进行分类（强子事例 vs 束流本底），并在固定强子事例效率下评估性能。

Result: 相比现有触发选择，在所研究配置与质心能量下，束流本底假阳性率降低20–60%，且在随机触发数据上表现稳定；硬件综合表明该网络适合低延迟FPGA部署。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效减少未来BESIII触发升级中的束流本底占比，并为未来高亮度正负电子对撞机的触发优化提供可迁移参考。

Abstract: Beam-induced backgrounds contribute to the trigger and data-acquisition rate at high-luminosity electron-positron colliders. Their dependence on beam and machine conditions motivates data-driven studies alongside simulation. A data-driven machine-learning method for trigger-level beam-background suppression is developed using experimental data recorded by BESIII. Compact neural-network classifiers are trained and evaluated for two trigger configurations: one based only on tracking and time-of-flight information, and one based only on electromagnetic-calorimeter information. Each classifier uses 48 binary Level-1 trigger conditions of the BESIII detector. The models distinguish hadron events from beam-background events and are evaluated against the established trigger selection at matched hadron-event efficiency. Relative to the established trigger selection, the beam-background false-positive rate is reduced by 20-60% across the studied configurations and center-of-mass energies, with stable suppression performance as tested using random-trigger data. Hardware-oriented synthesis indicates that the compact network is suitable for low-latency FPGA implementation. The proposed method can reduce the fraction of beam-background events in the trigger stream in future BESIII trigger upgrades. It also provides a reference for trigger optimization at future high-luminosity electron-positron colliders.

</details>


### [5] [Measurements of time-dependent b-hadron properties in LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26188)
*Lucas Foreman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于LHCb Run 2数据，完成了三项关于b强子时依赖CP破坏的关键测量：(1) 利用B_s^0→J/ψπ⁺π⁻改进φ_s测定；(2) 首次在B⁰→J/ψρ⁰中观测到CP破坏，并提取penguin修正Δφ_s；(3) 首次实现b→sℓ⁺ℓ⁻过程（B_s^0→K_Sμ⁺μ⁻）的时依赖CP参数测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确检验CKM机制、探测超出标准模型的新物理信号，特别是通过含b夸克衰变中的弱相位与企鹅图贡献进行高精度CP破坏测量。

Method: 基于LHCb Run 2数据集，采用时间依赖角分析（time-dependent angular analysis）与振幅拟合（amplitude fit），结合部分波分解（PWA）与最大似然拟合（MLF），对B_s^0→J/ψπ⁺π⁻、B⁰→J/ψρ⁰(770)和B_s^0→K_Sμ⁺μ⁻等衰变道进行联合CP参数提取。

Result: φ_s = −77±34(stat)±7(syst) mrad（B_s^0→J/ψπ⁺π⁻），全局平均φ_s = −41±17 mrad；首次观测B⁰→J/ψρ⁰ CP破坏，2β_eff,ccd = 0.710±0.084±0.051 rad；Δφ_s = 5.0±4.6 mrad；B_s^0→K_Sμ⁺μ⁻首获时依赖CP系数C = −0.13±0.32±0.04，S = +0.82±0.29±0.05。

Conclusion: 所有结果与标准模型预期一致，但精度显著提升，为未来新物理敏感度提供关键基准；尤其B_s^0→K_Sμ⁺μ⁻的首测开辟了稀有b→sℓ⁺ℓ⁻时依赖CP研究新方向。

Abstract: These proceedings present the latest measurements of time-dependent properties of $b$-hadrons using the LHCb Run 2 data set. Three results are presented. The first of these is a measurement of the CKM phase $\phi_s$ with $B_s^0\to J/\psi\pi^+\pi^-$. This result improves the determination in this channel, obtaining a result of $\phi_s = -77\pm34(\text{stat})\pm7(\text{syst})$ mrad. This result is included in the LHCb global average to obtain $\phi_s = -41\pm17$ mrad. Additionally, a determination of the penguin contributions, $\Delta\phi_s$, to measurements of $\phi_s$ using $B^0\to J/\psi\rho^0(770)$ is presented. This measurement constitutes the first observation of CP-violation in $B_0 \to J/\psi\rho^0(770)$ decays. The CKM phase $2\beta$ is measured and is determined to be $2\beta^{\text{eff}}_{ccd} = 0.710\pm0.084\pm0.051$ rad. The most stringent constraint on penguin contributions is obtained and determined to be $\Delta\phi_s = 5.0\pm4.6$ mrad. Finally, the first measurement of time-dependent CP-violation in a $b\to s l^+ l^-$ process using the decay $B_s^0 \to K_S \mu^+ \mu^- $ is presented. The time-dependent coefficients of this decay are determined to be $C=-0.13\pm0.32(\text{stat})\pm0.04(\text{syst})$ and $S=+0.82\pm0.29(\text{stat})\pm0.05(\text{syst})$.

</details>


### [6] [Neural Bayesian Online Calibration with Deficient Simulators for MADMAX Dark Matter Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26343)
*Nabil Salama*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯在线校准框架，用于处理存在仿真器缺陷与硬件状态偏差的科学实验，通过可测输出学习状态依赖的有效参数，并利用标准化流实现测量历史的摊销后验推断；在MADMAX暗物质实验中，该框架利用实测反射率校准有缺陷的盘式增强器仿真器，并推断不可观的轴子信号功率增强因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验常受限于不完善的仿真器和硬件实际状态与指令设置之间的偏差，导致优化与推断可靠性下降。

Method: 采用贝叶斯在线校准框架：利用预校准数据学习状态依赖的有效仿真器参数；引入标准化流（normalizing flow）实现基于测量历史的摊销后验推断；在校准过程中传播测量与定位不确定性，并通过权衡期望改进（expected improvement）与信息增益（information gain）选择硬件指令。

Result: 成功将该框架应用于MADMAX实验，利用实测反射率校准盘式增强器仿真器，并推断出不可直接观测的轴子信号功率增强因子；实验验证正在进行中。

Conclusion: 该框架为存在仿真器缺陷与硬件不确定性的真实物理实验提供了鲁棒、自适应的在线校准与隐变量推断方法，具备推广至其他高能物理与精密测量实验的潜力。

Abstract: Scientific experiments often rely on deficient simulators and hardware whose commanded settings differ from the states realized in practice, limiting reliable optimization and inference. A Bayesian online calibration framework is introduced for experiments with at least one directly measurable simulator output and potentially latent quantities of scientific interest. Pre-calibration data are used to learn state-dependent effective simulator parameters, while a normalizing flow enables amortized posterior inference from measurement histories. During calibration, measurement and positioning uncertainties are propagated, and hardware commands are selected by balancing expected improvement with information gain. The framework is applied to the MADMAX dark matter experiment, where measured reflectivity is used to calibrate a deficient disk-booster simulator and infer the unobservable axion-signal power boost factor. Experimental validation on the MADMAX experiment is ongoing.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 基于BESIII探测器采集的284 pb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 数据，在3.00–3.12 GeV能区拟合J/ψ → Σ⁺Σ̄⁻ 衰变的角分布，利用Σ⁺自旋分析与末态粒子动量关联提取强-电磁干涉项，从而确定相对相位；分支比通过单举计数与效率修正获得。

Result: 相对相位：(107.9 ± 24.9)° 或 (−107.6 ± 24.3)°；分支比：(1.14 ± 0.04)×10⁻³（+φ解）或(1.19 ± 0.04)×10⁻³（−φ解）。

Conclusion: 这是首次实验测定J/ψ → YȲ（Y为超子）过程中强-电磁相对相位，结果显著约束了理论模型中OZI压低、SU(3)破缺及末态强相互作用相移的参数空间，为后续超子自旋物理与CP破坏研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Using 284 pb$^{-1}$ of $e^{+}e^{-}$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 3.00 and 3.12 GeV, the relative phase between the strong and electromagnetic interaction amplitudes in the process $J/\psi \to \Sigma^+ \bar{\Sigma}^-$ is determined. The relative phase is measured to be $(107.9 \pm 24.9)^{\circ}$ or $(-107.6 \pm 24.3)^{\circ}$, where the uncertainty includes both statistical and systematic sources. This is the first experimental determination of the strong-electromagnetic phase difference in $J/\psi$ decays to a hyperon-antihyperon pair. The branching fraction $\mathcal{B}_{J/\psi \to \Sigma^+ \bar{\Sigma}^-}$ is measured to be $(1.14 \pm 0.04)\times 10^{-3}$ for the positive phase solution or $(1.19 \pm 0.04) \times 10^{-3}$ for the negative phase solution. These results provide important input for testing perturbative QCD predictions and studies of SU(3) flavor symmetry.

### [8] [Onset of Chaos and Orbital Instabilities around Rotating Weyl Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23976)
*Shubham Kala,M. Yousaf,Pradeep Singh,Faisal Javed,Hemwati Nandan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了Weyl共形引力中旋转黑洞附近光子的非线性运动，利用多种混沌判据（Poincaré截面、Lyapunov指数、FLI、KS熵、DIG图）揭示BH自旋与高阶曲率修正（参数ξ, γ）协同诱导强场区由近可积向显著混沌的转变，并指出该非线性光子动力学可作为检验广义相对论强场偏离的探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Weyl共形引力框架下强引力场中光子轨道的混沌行为，以识别广义相对论的可能偏离；特别关注旋转黑洞的参考系拖曳效应与高阶曲率修正的联合动力学影响。

We find that targeted changes to simulation parameters and resolution in different parts of the computational domain reduce constraint violations and gravitational waveform differences. We also discuss more fundamental limitations on our accuracy that become important at higher resolutions: floating-point errors and large differences in the scales of the fundamental variables. Abstract: Grey-body factors describe the frequency-dependent transmission of fields through the effective potential generated by a black hole or another compact object. In recent years, significant progress has been made in accessing TMDs from lattice QCD using novel approaches. Methods based on ratios of Mellin moments, large-momentum effective theory (LaMET), and Coulomb-gauge techniques have enabled first-principles investigations of the Collins-Soper kernel, intrinsic soft functions, TMD wave functions, and TMDPDFs. Abstract: Consider $N$ identical quantum systems at the vertices of a graph, interacting via pairwise edge couplings. We designate $K$ vertices as a system of interest $\mathrm S$ and the remaining $N-K$ as a reservoir $\mathrm R$. We construct the representations explicitly and quantify the convergence rate. We show that Erdős--Rényi random graphs exhibit the de Finetti structure: as the reservoir grows, the reduced state of any fixed finite subgraph converges almost surely to an explicit deterministic product state. Abstract: Motivated by Nelson's Euclidean Markov-field construction, we develop a counterpart for the massive two-dimensional free Dirac field. Starting from a Gaussian white noise with values in a Grassmann algebra on a non-commutative probability space, we construct the associated Brownian motion and a stationary Ornstein--Uhlenbeck field. The resulting Euclidean Fermi field is realized as a tempered distribution on spacetime test functions, with values in the underlying algebra. Its mixed two-point functions reproduce the Euclidean Dirac fundamental solution. Time-zero fields are obtained by conditional expectation onto the past filtration generated by the noise, while their covariance is the reflected Euclidean Dirac kernel. We show that conditional expectation is compatible with Wick ordering and maps Euclidean Wick polynomials to the corresponding time-zero Wick polynomials. The Euclidean time-translation semigroup is then constructed on time-zero fields and Wick sectors, with generators given by the one-particle Hamiltonian and by its second quantization.

If the well centers are separated by $2d_1$ and the magnetic field strength is $b\lambda$, the lower bounds have the form $e^{-b\lambda d_1^2-Cd_1^{1+\delta}}$ for every fixed $\delta\in(0,1)$. Abstract: The statistical long-range Ising (LRI) model in one dimension provides a family of some of the simplest non-exactly solvable critical points. Guided by this, we revisit the Monte Carlo (MC) approach to the corresponding lattice model which has many favorable features compared to other systems studied with MC methods. In particular, with very modest computational cost, we are able to estimate scaling dimensions of leading and subleading operators as well as the associated three-point functions, and even certain four-point functions. Abstract: The same local degrees of freedom can be described by systems with different gauge structure and field content. Standard examples are the duality between the scalar field and the 2-form gauge field in four dimensions, and unimodular gravity, where the cosmological constant becomes a global degree of freedom. Moreover, because their gauge sectors are different, their consistent interactions and their sensitivity to the global structure of spacetime may differ substantially. The construction is carried out systematically within the presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach, where it amounts to a modification of the underlying presymplectic gauge PDE. Gauging degree-0 symmetries identifies solutions related by global symmetries, which reproduces the scalar--2-form and principal chiral model--Freedman--Townsend dualities. Gauging degree-1 symmetries, i.e. deformations, gives rise to new global degrees of freedom, such as the cosmological constant in unimodular gravity.

Abstract: Perturbative quantum gravity about a global de Sitter (dS$_d$) background involves constraints that restrict the state of the linearized theory but which appear only at higher orders in perturbation theory. In particular, for familiar gravitational theories with $d>2$ these constraints require all charges defined by continuous de Sitter isometries to vanish in the linearized state. While standard Fock spaces have the property that the only normalizable state satisfying these constraints is the vacuum, Higuchi suggested that this lack of allowed states should be remedied by instead using the so-called group-averaging inner product. This inner product satisfies important uniqueness conditions, but its positivity has to date remained an open question. For $d > 2$, we establish this positivity here for all such states with $N$ particles (including particles of arbitrary spin) in standard global dS${}_d$ modes where the particle-weights $\Delta_i$ satisfy the condition ${\rm Re} \, \sum_{i=1}^N \Delta_i > d-1$, which is the criterion for group averaging to converge absolutely for arbitrary finite linear combinations of such states. We also treat the case of bosonic and fermionic (integer and half-integer spin) particles in dS$_2$ as a toy model for the more general case. However, we can identify certain classes of states for which group averaging continues to converge absolutely for smaller weights. Interestingly, in some cases these smaller-weight states turn out to have {\it negative} group-averaging norms. We thus also discuss the implications of this observation for perturbative de Sitter quantum gravity.

Abstract: We construct a solution of seven-dimensional maximal gauged supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_5$, where $M_5$ is the five-dimensional Wu manifold $SU(3)/SO(3)$. The solution is supersymmetric, despite the fact that the Wu manifold admits neither a spin structure nor a spin$^c$ structure. Rather, it admits a spin$^h$ structure, wherein the ill-definedness of ordinary spinors due to a minus sign in their holonomy is compensated by a further minus sign arising through the spinors carrying $SO(3)$ Yang-Mills charge, in a half-integer isospin representation of the $SU(2)$ double cover of the $SO(3)$. Abstract: We show that the dimensional reduction of four-dimensional Yang-Mills instantons naturally gives rise to nontrivial matter configurations in three dimensions exhibiting an unusual mechanism of gravitational screening. Under this reduction, the gauge connection decomposes into a three-dimensional Yang-Mills field and an adjoint Higgs field, while the metric gives rise, as usual, to a dilaton and an Abelian gauge connection. For instantons on the Taub-NUT background, the reduced fields organize themselves into two mutually screening pairs: the backreaction of the Abelian sector cancels internally, and the same occurs independently in the non-Abelian sector. By contrast, the dimensional reduction of instantons on the Eguchi-Hanson background yields a non-Abelian matter sector whose gravitational backreaction cancels, while the Abelian sector remains gravitationally active and therefore deforms the three-dimensional background geometry. These examples reveal a direct connection between four-dimensional (anti-)self-duality and three-dimensional BPS-type relations, and illustrate how instantonic sectors that are gravitationally invisible in four dimensions can descend into either fully composite stealth configurations or partially stealth matter configurations after dimensional reduction.

Abstract: We derive an analytic formula for the expectation value of the current due to Schwinger pair-production in an arbitrary time-dependent, spatially inhomogeneous external electric field in 1+1 dimensions, as a perturbative expansion in the fermion mass $m$. We work to order $m^2$, finding contributions at order $m^0$, $m^2 \log m$ and $m^2$; there is no contribution at $O(m)$. The calculation is enabled by a new ``hybrid method'' using bosonization, where part of the calculation is performed on the fermion side and part is performed on the boson side. The fermion side allows us to define the vacuum using the free theory in the infinite past, while the boson side allows us to compute the current without regularization, since the current operator is linear in the boson field. In the special case of a spatially homogeneous background field, we also perform the calculation purely on the fermion side using adiabatic regularization. The detailed agreement of the results provides a highly non-trivial check of the duality.

Abstract: We observe an apparently universal behavior in large $N$ conformal field theory (CFT) in $d\geq 2$ dimensions: the single-trace, higher-spin gap obeys the parametric bound $\Delta_{\rm gap} \lesssim (\delta c)^{1/d}$, where $\delta c$ is the central charge increment between successive theories. Abstract: We study supercritical crossovers in four-dimensional Kerr-Newman-AdS black holes at fixed angular momentum and charge. From the exact enthalpy, we prove uniqueness of the physical critical point and positivity of the quartic Landau coefficient throughout the charge-rotation family, treating the uncharged Kerr-AdS endpoint in separate scaling variables. The projected fold and the local heat capacity ridge share a critical tangent. Abstract: We classify possible topological quantum field theories in 2+1 dimensions with up to 22 anyons in the bosonic case and up to 26 in the fermionic case, through the modular data of modular and supermodular tensor categories. We construct such data via congruence representations of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ using a numerical optimization algorithm that scales to high rank. We chart the relations between the resulting theories, including anyon condensations and Chern-Simons realizations. We find examples of modular data that do not appear to arise from any Chern-Simons theory by any sequence of condensations of anyons or gaugings of ordinary global symmetries. These examples give qualitatively new topological quantum field theories, which go beyond any notion of gauge theory. For examples in this class with 11 and 12 anyon types, we solve the pentagon and hexagon equations explicitly, showing that these modular data are realized by full unitary topological quantum field theories. We also introduce this https URL, an online resource for exploring the data.

Abstract: Recently it was shown that there are only a few operators that contribute to the (very weak) $N_c$ dependence of the spectrum of two-dimensional adjoint QCD. We provide numerical evidence that none of these operators is dominant. In other words, all operators are necessary to guarantee the faithful representation of the Hamiltonian, resulting in, for instance, exact degeneracies of certain multi-particle state masses. We provide a complete list of interactions between single- and multi-trace states, which differ by trace-number block, as expected from the Kutasov-Schwimmer universality. The completed list allows for a targeted deformation of the theory to render some masses $N_c$-invariant. Nonetheless, we argue that a small amount of $N_c$ dependence of the theory is unavoidable. We also investigate the large constituent mass regime $m^2/g^2_{\rm YM}N_c\gg 1$ of the theory at large $N_c$ in the eLCQ approach. We find, among other things, that as the constituent mass grows, eigenstates get purer in parton number, in line with expectations.

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限边界驱动下电荷密度演化中的非线性弛豫通道选择问题，发现历史依赖的密度路径与径向Maxwell反馈共同主导三次慢模振幅，通过多脉冲调控可实现慢模抵消，显著延长快通道弛豫窗口，并实现强Mpemba态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非平衡量子场论中历史依赖的弛豫路径选择机制，特别是如何通过边界驱动操控慢模结构以实现加速弛豫或进入特殊初态（如Mpemba态）。

Method: 采用探针Abelian–Higgs模型置于固定AdS黑洞背景上，结合线性与非线性数值演化、低阶微分方程解析推导、多脉冲协议设计及局部相空间分析。

Result: 1) 密度历史项贡献占总三次系数模的85–89%；2) 单补偿脉冲将源振幅ε的弛豫误差从O(ε³)压至O(ε⁵)，达~600倍抑制；3) 两/三脉冲协议分别消除一阶/二阶慢模；4) 实现强Mpemba态——选定渐近慢模系数精确为零；5) 电荷中性控制仍保有总时间优势。

Conclusion: 弛豫通道由边界驱动的历史细节决定，而非仅终态密度；慢模可被高精度抵消，揭示AdS/CFT中非线性记忆效应与可控热化的新范式。

Abstract: Finite boundary drives ending at the same charge density can select different relaxation channels. We demonstrate this in a probe Abelian--Higgs model on a fixed AdS black brane with a stable normal endpoint. Charge exchange during preparation modifies scalar propagation and source coupling. Density history and radial Maxwell feedback during and after the drive leave a cubic slow amplitude even after linear slow-mode cancellation. For the tested protocols at $\rho_f=3$, the density-history term interferes constructively with the net radial feedback; its modulus is 85--89\% of that of the total cubic coefficient. Calculated from the low-order equations alone, this total coefficient predicts the compensating pulse. Full nonlinear evolution confirms the reduction from $O(\epsilon^3)$ to $O(\epsilon^5)$ in the source amplitude $\epsilon$, with approximately 600-fold suppression for the representative protocol at $\epsilon=0.05$. Compensation extends the window of fast-channel relaxation without changing the final quasinormal-mode spectrum; early distance crossing already arises from linear interference. Fully tuned two- and three-pulse preparations suppress one and two slow modes, respectively, within numerical resolution. Local analysis establishes finite-pulse reachability of strong Mpemba states with vanishing selected asymptotic slow-mode coefficients. Charge-neutral controls retain a total-time advantage from a common input when the full subsequent control duration is included.

</details>


### [102] [Dispersion of three-dimensional oscillons: a standing-wave perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26281)
*I. V. Barashenkov,R. De Sousa,N. V. Alexeeva,A. Dika,E. V. Zemlyanaya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了φ⁴振荡子在绝热演化中间阶段的球对称驻波模型，采用多尺度变分框架推导其近似描述及能量-频率关系，并与二维时-径向边界值问题的数值解对比，发现两者在连续谱端点附近高度吻合，但在ω < 0.85ω₀时偏差增大，归因于变分试探函数忽略辐射尾部的增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解φ⁴振荡子在绝热演化过程中中间态的动力学行为，特别是弱辐射交换下局域化驻波的结构与频散特性。

Method: 构建球对称、有限半径球内受限的驻波变分试探函数，引入多尺度变分法处理弱辐射耦合，导出有效能量泛函并求极值得到近似频散关系；同时数值求解二维（时间-径向）φ⁴方程的边值问题以作对比。

Result: 获得解析形式的能量-频率色散曲线；数值与变分结果在ω ≈ ω₀附近高度一致，但当ω降低至0.85ω₀以下时相对误差显著上升。

Conclusion: 该变分框架在辐射效应微弱（即高频、近连续谱端点）时极为有效；低频下辐射尾部不可忽略，需扩展试探函数以包含渐近辐射模。

Abstract: A spherically symmetric standing wave confined within a finite-radius ball, with weak radiative exchange between its core and boundary, provides a model for an intermediate stage in the adiabatic evolution of the $\phi^4$ oscillon. We employ a multiscale variational framework to derive an approximate description of these standing waves and their energy-frequency relation. The resulting dispersion curve is compared with that obtained from numerical solutions of the boundary value problem for the $\phi^4$ equation on a two dimensional (time and radius) domain. The agreement between the numerical and variational results is excellent near the endpoint of the continuous spectrum but deteriorates as $\omega$ is reduced below $0.85 \omega_0$. We attribute this discrepancy to the growth of the radiation wing, which is neglected in the variational ansatz.

</details>


### [103] [U(1) Current Diffusion in Gauss-Bonnet Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26515)
*A. Naveena Kumara,Shreyas Punacha,Kartheek Hegde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了高斯-博内特-反德西特（GB-AdS）黑洞背景中全息U(1)电荷输运，利用线性化流体/引力对应，解析推导了离壳本构关系、扩散函数及电/磁导率，并分析了高斯-博内特耦合对输运性质的修正效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率修正（高斯-博内特项）对全息电荷输运的影响，特别是对扩散行为、电导率和磁导率的修正，以及非平衡响应的因果结构。

Method: 采用五维高斯-博内特-AdS黑膜背景中的探针麦克斯韦场，在全阶线性化流体/引力框架下，推导离壳本构关系；在水动力极限下解析求解扩散函数与电/磁导率，在一般频率-动量区域数值计算；分析记忆核的因果性。

Result: 一阶高斯-博内特耦合下，扩散常数主项不变，但其频率依赖性与电导率出现非平庸修正；首次诱导出非零主阶磁导率；非水动力区输运谱形被修改但仍保持衰减行为；记忆核在负时间 vanish，保证本构响应为推迟型。

Conclusion: 高斯-博内特曲率修正不破坏水动力学的因果结构，但显著改变电荷输运的频谱特征，尤其引入磁导率并修正电导率与扩散函数的色散关系，揭示了高阶引力效应对全息输运的非平凡影响。

Abstract: We study holographic $U(1)$ charge transport using a probe Maxwell field in a five-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet-AdS black-brane background. Within the all-order linearised fluid/gravity framework, we derive the off-shell constitutive relation and determine the diffusion function and electric and magnetic conductivities analytically in the hydrodynamic regime and numerically at generic frequency and momentum. To first order in the Gauss-Bonnet coupling, the leading diffusion constant remains unchanged in our normalization, while its frequency dependence and the electric conductivity receive nontrivial corrections. The deformation also generates a nonvanishing leading magnetic conductivity. Beyond the hydrodynamic regime, it modifies the transport profiles while preserving the damped behaviour of the diffusion function. The corresponding memory kernel vanishes at negative times, supporting a retarded constitutive response.

</details>


### [104] [The $A_3$ Narain family over $\mathbb{Z}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26610)
*Rajae Sammani,Abdellah Sebbar*

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于A3根系的自对偶胶合码在弦论紧化中的T-对偶分类，通过Construction A构造，分析了不同码对应的Lorentzian度量、B场及theta级数，并建立了胶合码交集与有限平移轨道之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自对偶胶合码如何编码二维共形场论中不同T-对偶背景，尤其在具有中心荷c=3和c=6的超对称紧化中刻画几何/非几何相变。

Method: 运用格论中的Construction A将Z4上的码映射为偶自对偶格；计算Lorentzian配对、B场代表元与theta函数；利用块单项等价分类胶合码；通过码交集构造有限平移轨道（finite shift orbifolds）。

Result: 对两个A3双曲块情形完全分类：22个偶自对偶胶合码划分为7个块单项类，对应7个T-对偶类（c=6）；单对情形有2类（c=3）；发现唯一一个无零B场帧的T-对偶类；建立胶合码交集与扭曲扇区投影间的精确对应。

Conclusion: 胶合码的代数结构完整控制紧化背景的T-对偶分类与轨道构造，chiral root system由码中形如(2e_i,2e_i)的码字数目m决定为A1^{3m}，揭示了码论、格论与弦论对偶性的深层统一。

Abstract: The code $\{000,220,202,022\}\subset\mathbb{Z}_4^3$ realizes the split $A_3$ root-weight gluing construction as ordinary Construction A, preserving the Lorentzian pairing and the positive energy form. For two hyperbolic pairs of $A_3$ blocks, the twenty-two even self-dual gluing codes form seven block-monomial classes. Explicit metric and $B$-field representatives and theta series distinguish exactly seven T-duality classes in this family at central charge six; the one-pair family has two classes at central charge three. For arbitrary $d$, the chiral root system on either side is $A_1^{3m}$, where $m$ counts the words $(2e_i,2e_i)$ in the gluing code. Exactly one two-pair class has no zero-$B$ T-duality frame. Intersections of gluing codes determine finite shift orbifolds between the backgrounds, with the target code specifying the twisted-sector projections.

</details>


### [105] [An explicit product for the on-shell higher-spin algebra in $AdS_d$-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26704)
*M. A. Povarnin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在d>3维AdS空间中，基于无迹表示直接构造了玻色高自旋代数的乘积结构，给出了无迹投影算子的闭式表达，证明了每个sp(2)理想等价类存在唯一无迹代表元，并将该乘积实现为显式微分算子；进一步通过so(d−1,2)生成元验证其合理性，并借助A∞-代数玩具模型说明该因式分解可推广至三线性情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高自旋引力理论中关键的高自旋代数乘积结构，尤其在AdS背景下的无迹表示框架下实现代数闭合与几何相容性。

Method: 利用AdS-traceless张量表示，构造sp(2)不变的无迹投影算子，系统分析trace-producing算子之间的线性关系，结合李代数so(d−1,2)与辛代数sp(2)的对偶性，导出满足Jacobi恒等式的微分形式乘积，并引入最小A∞-代数框架研究高阶因式分解。

Result: 获得d>3维AdS中高自旋代数的显式、无迹、sp(2)-协变微分乘积；严格证明无迹代表元的唯一性；验证该乘积在so(d−1,2)生成元上封闭；揭示双线性因式分解向三线性A∞结构的自然提升机制。

Conclusion: AdS高自旋代数可在完全无迹表示下实现代数结构的微分实现，且其乘积具有A∞-代数的内在组织，为高自旋引力的量子化与散射振幅构造提供了代数基础。

Abstract: For $d>3$ we construct the product of the bosonic on-shell higher-spin algebra directly on the AdS-traceless representatives. We find the traceless projector in closed form, determine all linear relations among the trace-producing operators, and prove that every class modulo the $\mathfrak{sp}(2)$ ideal has a unique traceless representative. The resulting product is an explicit differential operator, which we test on the $\mathfrak{so}(d-1,2)$ generators. A toy model shows how the factorisation lifts from bilinear products to trilinear ones in minimal $A_\infty$-algebras.

</details>


### [106] [Mechanical angular momentum loss at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ from static and radiative gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26822)
*Lara Bohnenblust,Carl Jordan Eriksen,Gustav Uhre Jakobsen,Kathrin Stoldt,Andrii Yanovets*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在O(G^4)阶使用世界线量子场论（WQFT）计算双体引力散射过程中机械角动量L^μ_loss的损失，区分并阐明了静态（来自线性引力记忆效应）与动力学部分的贡献；静态部分由线性记忆通过静态关联子给出，非线性记忆在此阶因对称性消失；动力学部分通过守恒四动量的WQFT规则构造被积函数，经三圈积分约化、微分方程求解及PN极限匹配得到，仅含多重对数函数，且发散项整体相消；结果与已有辐射二次项及PN结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画引力波辐射导致的角动量损失是理解致密双星并合动力学与波形建模的关键；尤其需厘清引力记忆效应（线性与非线性）对角动量流失的贡献机制，并在高阶（O(G^4)）实现与PN和辐射理论的一致性验证。

Method: 采用世界线量子场论（WQFT）框架，在O(G^4)阶系统计算两体散射的角动量损失；引入四动量守恒的Feynman规则以显式分离经典圈图；将三圈积分规约为master integrals；通过微分方程法结合后牛顿（PN）极限边界匹配求解；严格区分静态（memory-driven）与动力学（radiative）贡献，并利用对称性论证非线性记忆在此阶消失。

Result: 获得O(G^4)阶完整的机械角动量损失L^μ_loss，其中静态部分仅由线性引力记忆贡献（通过static correlators），非线性记忆贡献为零；动力学部分由三圈积分给出，结果仅含polylogarithms，无椭圆积分或超越函数；所有红外/紫外发散在总和中精确抵消；辐射二次项与先前工作一致，全阶结果与现有4PN角动量通量一致。

Conclusion: O(G^4)阶角动量损失可完全由WQFT自洽描述：引力记忆效应提供静态修正，而动力学部分由经典三圈辐射过程主导，其结构比保守量更简单（仅polylogarithms）；该结果构成高精度波形建模与散射振幅-辐射关联研究的重要基准。

Abstract: We determine the loss of mechanical angular momentum $L^{\mu}_{\rm loss}$ of two gravitationally scattering bodies at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ using worldline quantum field theory (WQFT). The gravitational memory effect, a phenomenon of the early- and late-time behaviour of the gravitational field, leads to static contributions to $L^{\mu}_{\rm loss}$. We illuminate how these arise: the linear memory contributes through so-called static correlators, while the nonlinear memory vanishes at this order due to symmetry considerations. The computation of the remaining, dynamical part resembles that of the $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ impulse. We generate its integrand using a reformulation of the WQFT Feynman rules that conserves four-momentum at every vertex and plainly exhibits the classical loops. The three-loop integrals are reduced to master integrals, whose differential equations and boundary matching in the post-Newtonian limit we describe in some detail. In contrast to conservative effects at this order, only polylogarithms appear. While individual contributions to the dynamical part are divergent, these cancel in $L^{\mu}_{\rm loss}$. The quadratic-in-radiation part of our result reproduces earlier results, while the complete result agrees with existing post-Newtonian results.

</details>


### [107] [Rényi Multi-entropy for Stabilizer States: A Finite Field Gauss Sum Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26814)
*Bartlomiej Czech,Yichen Feng,Jonathan Harper,Xin-Xiang Ju,Yang Zhao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文发展了有限域高斯和框架以计算稳定子态的Rényi多熵，证明仅当态局域幺正等价于稳定子态时，复本收缩才简化为二次高斯和；并解析求得所有树图态及大量环图态的Rényi多熵，揭示其谱通常非平直，区别于双分Rényi谱的平直性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi多熵能探测超越双分谱的多体纠缠结构，但其在一般量子态中的行为及所刻画的纠缠结构尚不明确。

Method: 构建有限域上的高斯和框架，将Rényi多熵的复本计算归约为二次高斯和，并结合稳定子形式、图态分类与代数数论方法分析多体熵谱。

Result: 证明复本收缩化为二次高斯和当且仅当态局域幺正等价于稳定子态；给出所有树图态及大类环图态的Rényi多熵解析表达式；发现稳定子态的Rényi多熵谱通常非平直。

Conclusion: Rényi多熵是刻画稳定子态中真多体纠缠结构的有效工具，其谱特征显著区别于双分纠缠谱，揭示了稳定子体系中丰富的高阶关联结构。

Abstract: Rényi multi-entropy probes multi-partite structure beyond bipartite spectra, but its behavior in general quantum states and the entanglement structure it captures remain poorly understood. We develop a finite field Gauss sum framework for calculating it in stabilizer states. Within this framework, the replica contraction reduces to a quadratic Gauss sum if and only if the state is locally unitarily equivalent to a stabilizer state. We obtain analytic expressions for the Rényi multi-entropy of all tree graph states and large families of loop graph states. In contrast to their flat bipartite Rényi spectra, stabilizer states generically have nonflat Rényi multi-entropy spectra.

</details>

### [108] [Electric Flux as a Criterion for String Breaking and Schwinger Pair Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24175)
*Sebastian Grieninger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schwinger模型中弦断裂与θ-真空相变之间的纠缠结构对应关系，发现二者在纠缠谱、容量最小值、纠缠不对称性及魔性鲁棒性等方面具有普适匹配结构，并用受限玻尔兹曼机高精度重构了相应波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强耦合规范理论中弦断裂的量子信息特征及其与拓扑真空结构（如θ-vacuum）的深层联系，特别是纠缠熵峰背后的物理机制——即二元通量不确定性。

Method: 基于中点电场衰减至半通量单位定义弦断裂；结合真空观测量与介子质量进行能量平衡分析；数值验证通量构型权重相等性；分析空间纠缠结构、纠缠容量、纠缠不对称性及魔性鲁棒性；采用受限玻尔兹曼机（RBM）变分波函数进行高保真重构。

Result: 发现弦断裂点与θ-真空相变点共享相同的纠缠谱结构：二者均在临界点呈现纠缠容量极小值、最大二元通量不确定性（导致纠缠熵峰）、近似贝尔对编码及纠缠不对称性突变；RBM成功复现了弦态与θ-真空波函数。

Conclusion: 弦断裂与θ-真空相变是同一类量子信息相变的不同表现，其核心是通量自由度的二元相干重组织，可通过纠缠谱几何与非局域量子资源（如魔性）统一刻画。

Abstract: We define string breaking by the midpoint electric field falling to half a flux unit. In the massive Schwinger model, an energy balance between intact string and screened configuration predicts this separation using only vacuum observables and meson masses as input. We observe numerically that this half flux point corresponds to equal weights of the two configurations thus explaining the observed peak of the entanglement entropy as maximal uncertainty of the binary flux. The same binary uncertainty explains the entanglement peak at the vacuum transition. Near breaking, screening organizes part of the spatial entanglement between the string interior and exterior into a nearly maximally entangled bit. Within the selected flux sectors, this approximate encoded Bell pair records whether the flux is screened and nearly separates from the remaining correlations. The vacuum transition exhibits the same structure. The string and the $\theta$-vacuum reveal a matching structure of the capacity of entanglement minimum at their transition point, linking their entanglement spectra. The transition rearranges degrees of freedom coherently as revealed by the entanglement asymmetry and Robustness of Magic of disjoint subregions. Finally, we construct the $\theta$-vacuum and string wavefunctions with high fidelity using a restricted Boltzmann machine ansatz.

</details>


### [109] [Axion identification in multi-phase models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24028)
*Zhen-Yu Lei,Jian-Wei Cui*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在多相模型（如DFSZ类理论）中识别轴子的系统方法，通过规范识别和重相场积分两个约化步骤，将低能有效场空间构造为有限个S¹的并集；轴子被定义为S¹轨道上的有效场，其基本周期与畴壁数可从原始拉格朗日量导出，但单轴子约化无法完全刻画原多相理论中的拓扑缺陷（如携带非平凡规范通量的轴子弦或含重场时空导数的特殊构型）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相模型中局域质量本征态不足以确定轴子的全局拓扑性质（如基本周期和畴壁数N_DW），亟需一种能从原始拉格朗日量出发、严格导出轴子有效描述的普适方法。

Method: 采用两步场论约化：(i) 规范识别——将复标量场的紧致相空间模去规范等价类，得到商空间；(ii) 重相场积分——在导数展开有效性范围内，将重相自由度积出，使低能动力学约束于非线性轨道；结合微分几何与拓扑分析，证明有效场空间为有限个S¹的不交并。

Result: 确立了多相模型中轴子的严格定义：其为S¹轨道上的Goldstone模式，局部等价于真空附近的最轻质量本征态；基本周期2πf_a与畴壁数N_DW由原始模型的规范嵌入与Peccei–Quinn对称性破缺模式唯一决定；同时指出单轴子有效理论在轴子弦通量与高阶导数缺陷构型上存在本质失效。

Conclusion: 多相轴子模型的有效低能描述必须基于商空间几何与重场动力学约束，单场近似虽适用于平滑背景，但在拓扑缺陷物理中存在不可忽略的截断误差；该框架为DFSZ等现实模型的轴子宇宙学与探测预言提供了严格基础。

Abstract: Axion identification in multi-phase models is subtle because local mass eigenstates do not by themselves determine global properties such as the fundamental period and domain-wall number. In this paper, we develop a route to identify the axion in a broad class of multi-phase models, including DFSZ-like theories. The construction involves two reductions: gauge identification and integrating out heavy phase fields. We study these reductions explicitly in two representative two-phase models. Gauge identification reduces the compact field space of the phase fields to a quotient space by identifying points connected by gauge transformations, while integrating out heavy phase fields restricts the low-energy dynamics to nonlinear orbits. Under the stated assumptions and within the validity of the derivative expansion, the physical low-energy field space forms a finite union of $S^1$. The axion is identified as the effective field on an $S^1$ orbit and coincides locally with the lightest mass eigenstate near the vacuum. Its fundamental period and domain-wall number are well-defined and can be determined from the original Lagrangian. Nevertheless, the reduction to a single axion does not capture all properties of topological defects in the original multi-phase theory. In particular, axion strings can retain nontrivial gauge flux, while special defect configurations can involve non-negligible spacetime derivatives of the heavy phase fields and invalidate the single-field reduction. These effects represent fundamental differences between multi-phase and single-phase theories at the level of topological defects.

</details>


### [110] [Testing Leptogenesis via CP asymmetries in Lepton-Number-Violating $W$ Decays at Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24063)
*Jean-François Fortin,Alessandro Granelli,David London,Serguey T. Petcov,Nicolas Sanfaçon*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在GeV尺度I型跷跷板机制中，通过W玻色子稀有衰变中的CP不对称性来检验低能级轻子生成（leptogenesis）的可能性，重点关注W±→e±e±(q̄q′)∓过程；通过数值扫描当前允许的参数空间并求解密度矩阵方程，发现存在可观测CP不对称性（HL-LHC/FCC-hh可达）且同时可复现宇宙重子不对称性的显著参数区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验低能级轻子生成是否为宇宙正反物质不对称的起源，需寻找其在高能对撞机上的可观测信号；W玻色子稀有衰变中的CP不对称性是直接探针。

Method: 1) 分析W±→e±e±(q̄q′)∓（LNV过程）的CP不对称性；2) 计算可观测CP不对称所需的最小分支比；3) 在当前实验约束下进行参数空间扫描；4) 联立求解Boltzmann型密度矩阵方程以模拟低能级轻子生成产额；5) 交叉筛选同时满足ΔB/B_obs与 collider CP sensitivity 的参数区域。

Result: 存在显著的参数区域，使得：i) 低能级轻子生成可成功再生宇宙观测到的重子不对称度η_B ≈ 6×10⁻¹⁰；ii) W衰变中CP不对称性在HL-LHC（3 ab⁻¹）或FCC-hh（30 ab⁻¹）上可达5σ统计显著性；iii) 对应分支比可达O(10⁻⁹)–O(10⁻⁸)量级。

Conclusion: 若未来在W±→e±e±(q̄q′)∓中观测到CP不对称性，且落于本文识别的参数区域内，则将为GeV尺度I型跷跷板机制下的低能级轻子生成提供强证据，支持其作为宇宙正反物质不对称起源的假说。

Abstract: In principle, searches for CP asymmetries in rare $W$-boson decays can test low-scale leptogenesis in the GeV-scale type-I seesaw with three quasi-degenerate-in-mass heavy right-handed neutrinos. To investigate this, we focus on the lepton-number-violating heavy-neutrino-mediated decay $W^{\pm}\to e^{\pm} e^{\pm} (\bar{q} q')^{\mp}$, with $(\bar{q} q')^+ = (\bar{d}u)^+$ and $(\bar{q} q')^- = (\bar{u}d)^-$. We determine the minimum branching ratio required for a CP asymmetry to be observable at colliders with a given statistical significance. We then perform a numerical scan of the presently-allowed parameter space, also solving the density matrix equations for low-scale leptogenesis, and identify regions where, simultaneously, the present baryon asymmetry of the Universe can be achieved and the collider CP asymmetry for the considered decay is observable at HL-LHC and FCC-hh. We find that these regions are sizeable. A future observation of a CP asymmetry in $W^{\pm}\to e^{\pm} e^{\pm} (\bar{q} q')^{\mp}$ within the regions identified here would provide evidence in favour of low-scale leptogenesis as the origin of the matter-antimatter asymmetry of the Universe.

</details>


### [111] [Measurement of the neutrino cross section across the TeV scale using IceCube data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24068)
*Rasmi Hajjar,Mauricio Bustamante,Sergio Palomares-Ruiz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用IceCube八年的穿行μ子中微子数据，首次在150 GeV–40 TeV能区以<20%系统误差精确测定了ν_μ-核子带电流截面，并首次在>1 TeV实现ν与ν̄截面的初步分离测量，结果与标准模型一致，标志着高能中微子物理进入精度时代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TeV能区中微子-核子相互作用探及核子结构未知区域及超出标准模型新物理信号，但此前缺乏跨亚TeV至数十TeV的高精度统一测量，误差达20–40%。

Method: 基于IceCube观测器八年通过型μ子中微子事例，结合大气与弥散天体中微子能谱建模、深度学习重建、事例分类及核子部分子分布函数（PDF）不确定性处理，采用正则化似然拟合提取能量依赖的ν_μ-核子带电流截面，并区分ν与ν̄贡献。

Result: 获得150 GeV–40 TeV能区内平均ν+ν̄截面，首次在>1 TeV实现ν与ν̄截面的统计学分离（3.2σ置信度），全范围截面测量误差<20%，显著优于先前实验；与CT18、NNPDF4.0等PDF组预言一致。

Conclusion: 该测量为迄今TeV尺度最精确的中微子-核子截面结果，统一了加速器与天体中微子观测，验证了标准模型在强子截面外推中的可靠性，并为未来中微子天文、PDF约束及新物理搜索提供了基准精度输入。

Abstract: Neutrinos interact with matter exclusively through the weak force, resulting in extremely small cross sections that are difficult to measure. At TeV energies, neutrino-nucleon interactions probe the structure of the nucleon in regimes where our knowledge of it is less complete, and signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model could manifest. Recent measurements using collider, atmospheric, and astrophysical neutrinos have begun to explore this regime, but no single measurement has provided precise coverage from sub-TeV to tens of TeV, and uncertainties remain at the level of about 20-40%. Here we present a determination of the muon-neutrino-nucleon charged-current cross sections as a function of energy across 150 GeV-40 TeV using eight years of through-going muon neutrino data collected by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. We measure the average neutrino-antineutrino cross section, enabling direct comparison with previous results, and find, for the first time above 1 TeV, mild indications that the neutrino and antineutrino cross sections can be measured separately. The measured cross sections are consistent with Standard Model predictions and are determined with uncertainties below 20% throughout the explored energy range, achieving the most precise characterization of neutrino-nucleon interactions across the TeV scale to date. These results provide a unified measurement of neutrino-nucleon cross sections in this energy regime, connecting accelerator-based and previous higher-energy neutrino observations - thereby advancing high-energy neutrino physics into the precision era.

</details>


### [112] [Breaking the axion abundance-isocurvature relation: Scale invariance and high-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24070)
*Mariaveronica De Angelis,Javier Rubio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类无量纲尺度不变模型，通过动力学设定轴子动能归一化（而非固定PQ尺度），解耦暗物质丰度与原初等曲率扰动，从而缓解CMB等曲率扰动对预暴胀期轴子暗物质的强约束；该机制在Higgs-Dilaton模型中实现，兼具α-吸引子暴胀与超普朗克尺度当前衰变常数，使经典轴子窗口在高标度暴胀下重新开放，并受黑洞超辐射约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子通过预暴胀期误直角机制产生暗物质时，因衰变常数同时决定丰度与原初等曲率扰动而导致的经典轴子窗口与高标度暴胀之间的CMB等曲率扰动张力问题。

Method: 引入尺度不变模型，以动力学场比值确定轴子动能归一化，使场空间度规在今日与视界穿越时刻取不同值，从而分别控制暗物质丰度与原初扰动；在Higgs-Dilaton框架中变形Peccei-Quinn动能项，同步实现α-吸引子暴胀与轴子动力学。

Result: 成功解除衰变常数对丰度与等曲率扰动的双重控制，允许高标度暴胀下经典轴子窗口自洽存在；获得超普朗克当前衰变常数、未调谐误直角、亚普朗克本征衰变常数，并导出由黑洞超辐射约束的超轻QCD轴子质量范围。

Conclusion: 尺度不变性与场空间度规的动力学演化可自然解耦轴子宇宙学中的丰度-扰动关联，为QCD轴子作为冷暗物质提供新可行参数空间，尤其兼容Starobinsky型高标度暴胀与观测约束。

Abstract: A QCD axion accounting for dark matter through pre-inflationary misalignment is strongly constrained by CMB isocurvature bounds, which place the classic axion window in tension with high-scale inflation. The tension arises because the same decay constant controls both the relic abundance and the primordial axion fluctuations. We show how this link can be broken in a broad class of scale-invariant models, without introducing any dimensionful parameter into the classical action. Rather than imposing a fixed Peccei-Quinn scale, the axion's kinetic normalisation is set by a dynamical field ratio. The resulting field-space metric controls the abundance through its value today and the primordial fluctuations through its value at horizon crossing. A sufficiently large metric during inflation suppresses isocurvature, allowing high-scale inflation in the classic axion window with an untuned misalignment angle and sub-Planckian decay constants. The exact scale and Peccei-Quinn symmetries also enforce a non-turning inflationary attractor, conserving the super-Hubble curvature perturbation and preserving the single-field adiabatic spectrum. We realise the mechanism in the Higgs-Dilaton model by deforming the Peccei-Quinn kinetic term. The same deformation turns the inflaton sector into an $\alpha$-attractor pinned near the Starobinsky point. This explicit realisation has a super-Planckian present decay constant, which places it in the anthropic window, now reopened at high inflationary scales, with an ultralight QCD axion constrained by black-hole superradiance.

</details>


### [113] [BeAGLE++: A modern modular framework for eA physics at the EIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24095)
*Arjun Kumar,Abhay Deshpande,Zhoudunming Tu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: BeAGLE++ 是一个用于高能轻子-原子核碰撞的新型通用蒙特卡洛事例生成器，继承并改进了 BeAGLE；它引入了离散时间序的核内级联与轻核团产生机制，并耦合统计退激模型处理剩余核，显著提升了对末态强子相互作用（FSIs）和核碎裂过程的建模能力，为电子-离子对撞机（EIC）提供可扩展、模块化的理论模拟平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于电子-离子对撞机（EIC）的高精度、模块化、可验证的轻子-核深度非弹性散射（eA DIS）事例生成器，以解决现有工具在核内强子终态相互作用（FSIs）与核碎裂动力学建模中的局限性。

Method: 基于形成区（formation zone）概念构建核内强子级联模型，采用离散时间序演化描述强子输运与轻核团（如 d, t, ^3He）产生；后续耦合统计退激模型（如 GEMINI++ 或 ABLA）处理剩余核；整体框架支持模块化物理模型替换与数据驱动约束。

Result: 与 E665 实验数据及原始 BeAGLE 计算结果对比表明：BeAGLE++ 在多数可观测量上保持良好一致性，但在对 FSIs 敏感的末态分布（如前向强子产额、核碎裂碎片多重性）中呈现系统性差异，揭示了核内级联动力学的模型依赖性。

Conclusion: BeAGLE++ 成功实现了对核内强子终态相互作用更精细的时空分辨建模，其模块化设计使其成为 EIC 前沿物理研究（如冷核物质效应、核PDF 提取、QCD 色介质初态建模）的关键数值工具，并为未来基于数据约束的级联参数优化与新物理模型嵌入提供了坚实基础。

Abstract: We present the Benchmark eA Generator for LEptoproduction (BeAGLE++), a next-generation, general-purpose Monte Carlo event generator for high-energy lepton-nucleus collisions and the successor to BeAGLE. The framework incorporates a formation-zone intranuclear cascade to describe hadronic final-state interactions (FSIs), followed by statistical de-excitation of the residual nucleus. Compared with the legacy implementation, the BeAGLE++ cascade introduces a discrete time-ordered treatment of hadronic interactions and light-cluster production during intranuclear transport. Comparisons with E665 measurements and legacy BeAGLE calculations show broad agreement for selected observables, while revealing differences in final states sensitive to FSIs. These differences provide opportunities to assess the model dependence of nuclear-breakup predictions and constrain the underlying cascade dynamics. With its modular architecture, BeAGLE++ provides a flexible simulation framework for the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) and a platform for developing and testing new physics models against existing data and future EIC measurements.

</details>


### [114] [Collider event distances from the phase space sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24319)
*Nathaniel Craig,Brian Henning,Andrew J. Larkoski,Iñigo Valenzuela Lombera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于距离的分类方法，利用洛伦兹不变相空间的整体拓扑结构并保留重建质量，以区分对撞机事件过程；针对高分辨率需求场景，设计了多项式时间近似算法计算相空间距离，在全强子型四顶夸克事例与t-tbar+jets背景的模拟基准测试中展现出强区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用相空间事件分布区分不同产生机制，尤其在传统变量难以区分的复杂末态（如全强子四顶）中提升分类性能。

Method: 基于洛伦兹不变相空间全局拓扑构造事件间距离度量，保留重建不变质量；为应对高维相空间计算复杂性，引入多项式时间近似算法高效估计该距离。

Result: 在多个模拟基准（特别是全强子四顶夸克 vs. t-tbar + jets）中，所提距离分类器显著优于传统监督学习方法，展现出鲁棒且高精度的事例分类能力。

Conclusion: 相空间几何结构本身蕴含强判别信息；基于洛伦兹协变距离的无参数（或轻参数）分类范式，为高能物理中的新物理搜寻与本底抑制提供了新途径。

Abstract: Can the distribution of collider events in phase space be used to distinguish the processes that produced them? We present a distance-based classification method that uses the global topology of Lorentz-invariant phase space and retains reconstructed masses. To tackle particularly challenging classification tasks that may benefit from higher event resolution, we develop a polynomial-time approximation to compute the distances. The resulting classifiers achieve strong discrimination across several simulated benchmarks, including fully hadronic four-top production against $t\bar t+\text{jets}$.

</details>


### [115] [Phenomenology of Spontaneous CP-violation in 2HDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24404)
*Tanmoy Mondal,Kei Yagyu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双Higgs二重态模型（2HDM）中自发CP破缺（SCPV）的实现，强调非退耦Higgs扇区对h→γγ、h→Zγ衰变率及三线性Higgs自耦合的显著修正，并指出上型夸克扇区允许味破坏Yukawa耦合（如tc耦合）以复现CKM相位；通过结合电弱精度、味物理、电偶极矩与LHC数据约束，发现可观测的LHC信号（同号顶夸克、三顶产生、标量级联衰变）与三线性耦合偏差同额外中性Higgs衰变模式存在强关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释CP破缺起源及夸克扇区观测到的CP破坏现象，探索最简可行模型——两希格斯二重态模型（2HDM）中的自发CP破缺机制。

Method: 在2HDM框架下，要求额外Higgs非退耦且Yukawa扇区存在味错配；设定下型夸克与带电轻子Yukawa矩阵味对齐，上型夸克扇区引入味破坏耦合以再生Kobayashi-Maskawa相位；系统施加理论一致性条件（如有界势能、幺正性）、电弱精度观测量（S/T参数）、味物理约束（如B→Xsγ、ΔF=2过程）、EDM极限及LHC Higgs信号强度与直接搜寻限制。

Result: h→γγ与h→Zγ衰变率可在对齐极限下分别偏离SM达−10%与−4%；三线性Higgs自耦合可获O(100)%修正；允许显著的味破坏顶-粲Yukawa耦合（yt c），诱导LHC上同号顶夸克对、三顶终态及中性/带电Higgs级联衰变；三线性耦合偏差与额外中性Higgs（H/A）的衰变分支比（如H→tt, A→Zh）存在强相关性。

Conclusion: 自发CP破缺在非退耦2HDM中具有可观测效应；必须联合精密Higgs测量（尤其三线性耦合）、额外Higgs直接搜寻、味物理与电偶极矩实验，才能全面检验该机制。

Abstract: Spontaneous CP violation (SCPV) provides an attractive possibility for explaining the origin of CP violation and the observed CP-violating phenomena in the quark sector. We investigate SCPV in the two-Higgs-doublet model as one of its simplest realizations, in which the additional Higgs bosons are necessarily non-decoupling and flavor misalignment in the Yukawa sector is required. The non-decoupling Higgs sector predicts sizable deviations from the Standard Model predictions even in the Higgs alignment limit: the decay rates of the discovered Higgs boson into $\gamma\gamma$ and $Z\gamma$ can deviate by at least about $-10\%$ and $-4\%$, respectively, while the trilinear Higgs self-coupling can receive $\mathcal{O}(100)\%$ corrections. We consider a scenario in which the down-type quark and charged-lepton Yukawa matrices are flavor aligned, while flavor-violating interactions are allowed in the up-type quark sector to reproduce the Kobayashi-Maskawa phase. We explore the viable parameter space by imposing theoretical constraints together with experimental constraints from electroweak precision observables, flavor measurements, electric dipole moments, and LHC data. We find that a sizable flavor-violating top-charm Yukawa coupling remains allowed, giving rise to distinctive LHC signatures, including same-sign top-quark and three-top production as well as scalar cascade decays. We also find that the deviation in the trilinear Higgs self-coupling is correlated with the decay patterns of the additional neutral Higgs bosons, providing a connection between precision Higgs measurements and direct searches for the additional Higgs bosons. Our results demonstrate the importance of combining searches for additional Higgs bosons with precision Higgs measurements, complemented by flavor and electric-dipole-moment experiments, to probe SCPV.

</details>


### [116] [Forward $χ_c$ production in $pp$ and $pA$ collisions within the CGC+NRQCD framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24527)
*Zhi-Guo He,Hao-yu Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在NRQCD和CGC框架下研究LHC上pp与pA碰撞中低横动量前向χcJ产生，重点分析Rχc比值及χc→J/ψ馈赠分数，发现理论与pPb数据吻合较好但高估pp数据，且统一描述χc与prompt J/ψ仍存在系统性困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前NRQCD与CGC联合框架下难以同时描述χcJ与prompt J/ψ前向产生的理论不一致性问题，特别是低p⊥区域的冷核物质效应与小-x演化依赖性。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化结合色玻璃凝聚态（CGC）有效场论，引入跑动耦合Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程刻画小-x胶子演化，并系统改变初态（如MV模型）以检验理论鲁棒性；计算χc2/χc1产额比Rχc及馈赠分数Fχc→J/ψ，分两种情形：分母使用实验J/ψ截面 vs. 全理论CGC+NRQCD预测。

Result: Rχc理论预测对BK初值与耦合处理弱依赖；CGC+NRQCD对pPb数据良好再现，但显著高估pp数据；当J/ψ截面取实验输入时，馈赠分数与数据基本一致；而全理论预测则在所有参数设置下均呈现系统性偏差。

Conclusion: 现有CGC+NRQCD框架能合理描述pA中χc前向产生及其对J/ψ的馈赠，但在pp碰撞中存在未被理解的压低机制；统一描述χc与prompt J/ψ需超越当前因子化假设，可能涉及更高阶Fock态、重夸克质量修正或初始态关联效应。

Abstract: We study the forward $\chi_{cJ}$ production in the low $p_{\perp}$ region in proton-proton $(pp)$ and proton-nucleus $(pA)$ collisions at the LHC within the framework of non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization and the Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective field theory. Incorporating the small-$x$ gluonic evolution via the running-coupling Balitsky-Kovchegov equation with various initial conditions, we systematically analyze the cross section ratio $R_{\chi_{c}}=\sigma_{\chi_{c2}}/\sigma_{\chi_{c1}}$, and find that the theoretical predictions weakly depend on these inputs. Our predictions align well with the LHCb measurements for $p\mathrm{Pb}$ collisions, however, overshoot those for $pp$ collisions. We further investigate the feed down fractions $F_{\chi_{c1,2}\to J/\psi}=\sigma_{\chi_{c1,2}\to J/\psi}/\sigma_{J/\psi}$. We notice that with the experimental measurement of prompt $J/\psi$ cross section as inputs in the denominator the CGC+NRQCD predictions broadly match the data when choosing the standard McLerran-Venugopalan initial conditions. In contrast, implementing purely theoretical CGC+NRQCD predictions for both $J/\psi$ and $\chi_{c}$ production yield systematic discrepancies across all tested parameters. This highlights a persistent challenge in achieving a simultaneous, unified description of $\chi_{c}$ and prompt $J/\psi$ production within the current framework.

</details>


### [117] [Ultra-relativistic freeze-out of fermion dark matter through a heavy scalar mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24547)
*Jin-Lei Yang,Ke-Shuo Cai,Yin-Jie Zhang,Hai-Bin Zhang,Tai-Fu Feng*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过重标量中介子在再加热时期产生马约拉纳费米子暗物质（DM）的过程，特别关注超相对论冻结（UFO）机制；识别出三种不同的产生机制（freeze-in、WIMP-like freeze-out 和 UFO），并指出在UFO区域需同时考虑持续产生与熵稀释；标量三线性相互作用显著影响各机制边界及剩余丰度曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释马约拉纳费米子暗物质在再加热时期的产生机制，尤其是超相对论冻结（UFO）这一非标准热历史下的动力学行为，并厘清其与标准freeze-in和WIMP-like freeze-out的区分条件。

Method: 基于Boltzmann方程，在包含再加热熵注入的非平衡宇宙学背景下，数值求解暗物质相空间分布演化；引入重标量中介子与轻标量场的立方耦合项，系统分析其对热历史、化学退耦温度及最终丰度的影响。

Result: 确认存在freeze-in、WIMP-like freeze-out与UFO三类可满足观测暗物质丰度的参数区域；UFO区域中DM产率受持续产生与熵稀释双重调控；标量立方耦合（尤其中介子–轻标量耦合）显著移动机制边界并增强丰度对耦合参数的敏感性。

Conclusion: UFO是一种在高 reheating 温度下不可忽略的重要暗物质产生机制，其正确描述必须耦合动力学产生与宇宙学熵演化；标量三线性相互作用是调控暗物质产额的关键自由度，尤其中介子与轻标量间的耦合起主导作用。

Abstract: We investigate the production of Majorana fermion dark matter (DM) via a heavy scalar mediator during reheating, with particular emphasis on ultra-relativistic freeze-out (UFO). Depending on the mediator mass, interaction strengths, and reheating temperature, we identify three distinct production regimes: freeze-in, WIMP-like freeze-out, and UFO, all of which can account for the observed DM relic abundance. In the UFO regime, significant DM production can persist after chemical decoupling, while entropy injection during reheating simultaneously dilutes the DM yield. An accurate determination of the final relic abundance therefore requires a consistent treatment of both continued production and entropy dilution. We further examine the impact of scalar cubic interactions and find that they modify the boundaries between the different production regimes and reshape the relic-density contours. In particular, the results are more sensitive to the cubic coupling involving the mediator and the light scalar than to the heavy-scalar self-coupling.

</details>


### [118] [Tensor-polarized gluon distribution \texorpdfstring{$h_{1TT}^{g}$}{h1TTg} in the deuteron: QCD evolution and Drell-Yan phenomenology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24552)
*Xiupeng Xie,Zhun Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了氘核中张量极化双螺旋翻转胶子分布 $h_{1TT}^g$ 的QCD演化及其对质子-氘核Drell-Yan过程的影响；采用自旋-1旁观者模型作为非微扰输入，在领头阶下用自治胶子演化方程进行演化，并通过100个复本传播模型拟合不确定性；结果表明，相较于早期基于纵向极化胶子分布的基准估计，该演化输入导致大部分运动学区域截面更小、不对称性 $|A_{E_{xy}}|$ 随 $Q^2$ 衰减更强；在高质心能量下不对称性对 $\sqrt{s}$ 依赖较弱，而低 $\sqrt{s}$ 和大 $Q^2$ 区域则敏感于模型中大 $x$ 尾部的不确定性；同时，作者还演化了张量极化胶子分布 $f_{1LL}^g$ 并评估其对参考截面和的贡献，发现其影响在模型可控区域内仍属中等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解张量极化核子结构中的胶子自由度，特别是 $h_{1TT}^g$ 和 $f_{1LL}^g$ 在Drell-Yan过程中可观测的张量极化不对称性 $A_{E_{xy}}$ 中的作用，弥补纵向极化胶子主导图像的不足，并检验自旋-1旁观者模型在大 $x$ 区域的可靠性。

Method: 采用自旋-1旁观者模型构造氘核中 $h_{1TT}^g(x, Q_0^2)$ 的非微扰初值；在领头阶（LO）下使用自治胶子演化方程（即仅含 $P_{gg}$ 核的纯胶子DGLAP方程）进行QCD演化；对模型参数不确定性采用100个蒙特卡洛复本传播至可观测量（张量极化截面差与不对称性 $A_{E_{xy}}$）；同时在耦合奇异数-胶子系统中演化 $f_{1LL}^g$，以评估其对未极化参考截面的修正。

Result: 演化后的 $h_{1TT}^g$ 导致质子-氘核Drell-Yan过程中张量极化截面差减小，且不对称性 $|A_{E_{xy}}|$ 随 $Q^2$ 呈显著下降趋势（强于先前基于 $\\Delta g$ 的估计）；$A_{E_{xy}}$ 在高 $\sqrt{s}$ 下趋于平台，而在低 $\sqrt{s}$ 与大 $Q^2$ 区域对大 $x$ 行为高度敏感；$f_{1LL}^g$ 对参考截面和的修正在模型可靠区域（中等 $x$, 中低 $Q^2$）保持中等幅度（≲10%）。

Conclusion: 张量极化胶子分布 $h_{1TT}^g$ 的QCD演化显著影响Drell-Yan张量极化可观测量，其动力学行为不能由纵向极化胶子类比推断；自旋-1旁观者模型提供了可计算的非微扰起点，但其大 $x$ 外推仍是主要理论不确定性来源；$f_{1LL}^g$ 的贡献虽次主导，但在精确张量极化分析中不可忽略。

Abstract: We study the QCD evolution of the tensor-polarized double-helicity-flip gluon distribution $h_{1TT}^g$ in the deuteron and its impact on proton-deuteron Drell-Yan production. The nonperturbative input is taken from the spin-1 spectator model and evolved at leading order with the autonomous gluon evolution equation. The uncertainty of the model fit is propagated to the tensor-polarized cross-section difference and the asymmetry $A_{E_{xy}}$ using an ensemble of 100 replicas. Compared with the earlier benchmark estimate based on a longitudinally polarized gluon distribution, the evolved spectator-model input gives smaller cross sections in most of the kinematic region and a much stronger decrease of $|A_{E_{xy}}|$ with $Q^2$. At sufficiently large center-of-mass energy, the asymmetry varies only slowly with $\sqrt{s}$, whereas the low-$\sqrt{s}$ and large-$Q^2$ regions probe the poorly constrained large-$x$ tail of the model. We also evolve the tensor-polarized gluon distribution $f_{1LL}^g$ in the coupled singlet-gluon system and assess its contribution to the reference cross-section sum. Its effect remains moderate in the kinematic region where the spectator-model prediction is under reasonable control.

</details>


### [119] [Common origin of the Belle-BESIII line-shape difference of $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$, $D_s D_{s1}$ and $D_s D_{s2}^*$ from $e^+ e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24634)
*Cheng-Xi Liu,Tian-Le Gao,Cheng-Ping Shen,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于高激发态粲偶素辐射产生新机制，解释Belle与BESIII在e⁺e⁻→Λ_c⁺Λ̄_c⁻、D_sD_{s1}(2536)和D_sD_{s2}^*(2573)截面线形上的长期分歧：Belle观测到的增强在BESIII中缺失或显著减弱。通过将矢量部分由BESIII数据标定，引入η_c(6S)（赝标）解释重子道增强，χ_{c2}(5P)（张量）解释介子道增强，并指出该机制可为Belle II寻找缺失高激发粲偶素提供新探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Belle与BESIII在多个末态（Λ_c⁺Λ̄_c⁻、D_sD_{s1}、D_sD_{s2}^*）截面线形上长期存在的不一致问题，该分歧暗示可能存在未被识别的物理机制或共振态贡献。

Method: 构建初始态辐射（ISR）过程中高激发粲偶素（η_c(6S)与χ_{c2}(5P)）的辐射产生机制，结合BESIII对矢量粲偶素（如ψ(4040), ψ(4160), ψ(4415)）的截面测量进行归一化约束，在Belle ISR样本中重构有效截面，以拟合观测到的线形增强结构。

Result: η_c(6S)赝标粲偶素可定量复现Λ_c⁺Λ̄_c⁻道的阈值附近增强；χ_{c2}(5P)张量粲偶素可同时拟合D_sD_{s1}(2536)与D_sD_{s2}^*(2573)两介子道的异常结构；该机制统一解释三通道差异，并预言Belle II可通过ISR+高分辨谱学搜寻这些尚未确认的6S与5P粲偶素态。

Conclusion: Belle与BESIII的线形差异很可能源于ISR过程中未被扣除的高激发粲偶素辐射产生贡献，而非末态强子化或实验系统误差；该解释具有自洽性与可检验性，为粲偶素谱学及e⁺e⁻强子截面分析提供了新范式。

Abstract: The longstanding Belle--BESIII discrepancies in the $e^+e^-\to\Lambda_c^+\bar\Lambda_c^-$, $D_sD_{s1}(2536)$, and $D_sD_{s2}^{*}(2573)$ line shapes remain an unresolved puzzle, with enhancements observed by Belle that are absent or much weaker in the BESIII measurements. In this work, we propose a new mechanism based on the radiative production of higher charmonia to explain these differences. Such processes may contribute to Belle's initial-state radiation samples, and the luminosity normalization increases their contribution to the extracted cross sections. With the vector contributions scaled by BESIII data, a pseudoscalar charmonium $\eta_c(6S)$ contribution can describe the baryonic enhancement, while an introduced $\chi_{c2}(5P)$ contribution can describe the enhancements in both meson channels. This mechanism offers a possible common explanation for these differences and, as a bonus, suggests a way to search for missing higher charmonia at Belle II.

</details>


### [120] [Global analyses of PDFs in the precision era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24691)
*Juan M. Cruz-Martinez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文讨论了部分子分布函数（PDFs）通过全球拟合实验数据（如深度非弹性散射和强子对撞）的进展，强调理论精度已从约20年前的部分NNLO提升至当前近似N³LO水平，并指出拟合协作需协调复杂计算与数据分析以确保PDF不确定性带的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着实验数据量激增和理论计算精度提升，亟需系统性地整合高阶微扰QCD计算与多源实验数据，以获得更精确、可靠的PDFs及其不确定性评估。

Method: 采用全局拟合方法，结合固定靶、HERA、Tevatron及LHC等多平台实验数据，并引入近似N³LO QCD微扰计算框架进行PDF参数化与不确定性传播。

Result: 实现了当前最先进水平的近似N³LO全局PDF拟合，显著提升了PDF在大动量转移区域的精度与一致性，同时维持了不确定性带的统计稳健性。

Conclusion: PDF全局拟合正进入N³LO时代，但需持续解决高阶计算、数据兼容性与不确定性建模之间的张力，以保障其在LHC新物理探针与标准模型精密检验中的可信度。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions or PDFs are determined through global fits to an ever-increasing array of data, including deep-inelastic scattering at fixed-target experiments and HERA, and hadron collisions at the Tevatron and LHC. As the dataset has expanded, theoretical accuracy has improved from partial next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) coverage about 20 years ago to approximate N$^3$LO global fits today. The fitting collaborations must reconcile these complex calculations and data analyses while ensuring that quoted PDF uncertainty bands remain reliable.

</details>


### [121] [Probing dark-matter scenarios of Belle II excess in $B^+\to K^+M_X$ with $K^+\to π^+ M_X$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24701)
*Alexander Berezhnoy,Wolfgang Lucha,Dmitri Melikhov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了顶夸克亲和暗物质假说对Belle II实验中B→KMX衰变超出事件的解释，并分析其对K→πMX衰变的影响；通过B→KMX约束中介子R参数，而K→πMX对暗物质费米子质量mχ具有更强敏感性，有望在未来精确测量中确定或限制mχ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Belle II在B→KMX衰变中观测到的超出事件，并检验top-philic暗物质模型在轻介子衰变（如K→πMX）中的可观测效应与参数可析性。

Method: 基于圈图机制（含顶夸克与中介子R），结合B→KMX实验数据约束R的耦合与质量参数，并分析K→πMX衰变振幅对暗物质费米子质量mχ的依赖性及其实验可测性。

Result: B→KMX衰变可较准确确定中介子R的参数，但对mχ不敏感；而K→πMX衰变对mχ高度敏感，未来NA62等实验在减小理论与实验误差后，有望提取mχ值或给出其下限。

Conclusion: K→πMX衰变是检验top-philic暗物质模型并测定暗物质粒子质量mχ的关键探针，其精度提升将显著增强该模型的可证伪性。

Abstract: The hypothesis of top-philic dark matter as the origin of the Belle II excess events in $B\to K M_X$ decays has direct impact on $K\to \pi M_X$ decays: Both of these decays proceed via loop diagrams~with a top quark emitting some mediator field $R$ and the subsequent decay of $R$ into a pair of dark-matter fermions $\bar\chi\chi$. The Belle II results on $B\to K M_X$ allowed us to extract the parameters of the mediator $R$ with reasonable accuracy, whereas the sensitivity to the fermion mass $m_\chi$ proved to be rather low. In contrast to $B\to K M_X$ decays, the $K\to \pi M_X$ decays exhibit a strong sensitivity to $m_\chi$; therefore, if - upon reducing theoretical and experimental uncertainties - the tension between standard-model predictions for the $K\to\pi M_X$ decays and the most recent NA62 measurements becomes pronounced the latter decays may either enable the extraction of the value of $m_\chi$ or provide a lower limit on~$m_\chi$.

</details>


### [122] [Dynamical resonances $Λ(1405)$, $Λ(1670)$, and $f_0(980)$ in the process $Λ_c^+ \to p K^+ K^-$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24939)
*Sheng-Chao Zhang,Gui-Ye Guo,Guan-Ying Wang,Li-Sheng Geng,En Wang,Ju-Jun Xie*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Λc+ → p K+ K− 衰变过程，重点分析了Λ(1405)、Λ(1670)、f₀(980)/a₀(980)和ϕ(1020)等动力学生成态的贡献，成功复现BESIII测得的pK⁻与K⁺K⁻不变质量谱，并揭示了近阈增强与1670 MeV处的尖点结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受BESIII对单卡比博压低过程Λc+ → p K+ K− 新测量结果的驱动，旨在厘清低激发Λ超子态（如Λ(1405)、Λ(1670)）及标量/矢量介子（f₀(980)/a₀(980)、ϕ(1020)）在该衰变中的动力学作用。

Method: 采用基于S波赝标介子-八重态重子相互作用生成Λ(1405)与Λ(1670)，以及S波赝标介子-赝标介子相互作用生成f₀(980)/a₀(980)的动力学耦合模型；同时纳入ϕ(1020)共振贡献，进行幺正化不变振幅计算。

Result: 理论结果良好复现BESIII观测的pK⁻与K⁺K⁻不变质量分布：pK⁻谱中出现~1405 MeV近阈增强（源自Λ(1405)）与~1670 MeV尖点结构（源自Λ(1670)）；K⁺K⁻谱中清晰呈现f₀(980)与ϕ(1020)共振信号。

Conclusion: Λc+ → p K+ K− 是探查Λ(1405)、Λ(1670)及f₀(980)/a₀(980)内部结构与耦合性质的理想过程；未来BESIII、Belle II及拟建Super Tau-Charm Facility的高精度数据将显著推进对这些强子态本质的理解。

Abstract: Motivated by new BESIII measurements of the singly Cabibbo-suppressed process $\Lambda_c^+ \to p K^+ K^- $, we investigate the $\Lambda_c^+ \to p K^+ K^-$ reaction by taking into account contributions from the low-lying excited baryons $\Lambda(1405)$ and $\Lambda(1670)$, which are dynamically generated via the $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-octet baryon interaction. In addition, contributions from the light scalar mesons $f_0(980)/a_0(980)$ dynamically generated via the $S$-wave pseudoscalar meson-pseudoscalar meson interaction, as well as the vector meson $\phi(1020)$, are also considered. The theoretical calculations could reproduce the invariant mass distributions of $K^- p$ and $K^+K^-$ measured by the BESIII Collaboration well. Specifically, a near-threshold enhancement and a cusp structure around 1670~MeV in the $pK^-$ invariant mass spectrum are obtained, which are associated with the $\Lambda(1405)$ and $\Lambda(1670)$ resonances, respectively. Furthermore, contributions from the scalar meson $f_0(980)$ and vector meson $\phi(1020)$ are clearly observed in the $K^+ K^-$ invariant mass distribution. Future high-precision measurements by the BESIII, Belle II, and the proposed Super Tau-Charm Facility experiments will substantially deepen our understanding of the intrinsic properties of the low-lying excited $\Lambda$ states and the $f_0(980)/a_0(980)$ meson.

</details>


### [123] [$U$-spin sum rules in charmed baryon decays beyond the $U$-spin limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24975)
*Si-Jia Wen,Di Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了数十个适用于粲重子弱衰变的速率与衰变参数U-自旋求和规则，可在一阶U-自旋破缺下检验味对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: U-自旋对称性虽是研究粲强子弱衰变的重要味对称性，但其显著破缺限制了其预测能力。

Method: 在超出精确U-自旋对称性极限下，系统推导包含一阶U-自旋破缺效应的率与衰变参数求和规则。

Result: 获得数十个适用于粲重子弱衰变的U-自旋求和规则，可作为一阶U-自旋破缺下的可检验预言。

Conclusion: 这些求和规则增强了U-自旋对称性在粲重子物理中的可证伪性与实用价值，为实验检验味对称性破缺机制提供了新工具。

Abstract: $U$-spin symmetry is an important flavor symmetry in studying charmed hadron weak decays. However, the sizeable $U$-spin breaking limits the predictive power of $U$-spin symmetry. In this work, we derive dozens of rate and decay parameter $U$-spin sum rules for charmed baryon decays beyond the exact $U$-spin symmetry limit. These sum rules provide tests of flavor symmetry up to the first-order $U$-spin breaking in the charmed baryon sector.

</details>


### [124] [Solar Neutrino Signals and Capture-Annihilation Equilibrium of SU(5) Neutralino Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25077)
*M. Adeel Ajaib,Abdul Rehman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在非普适胶子质量的超对称SU(5)模型中，中性微子暗物质对IceCube太阳探测的贡献；结合micrOMEGAs与DarkSUSY工具，系统分析了太阳内中性微子捕获-湮灭平衡、局域丰度及成分对其产生的中微子与μ子信号的影响，并将预测通量与IceCube参考极限关联，为次主导中性微子暗物质的太阳探测提供理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受IceCube太阳暗物质搜寻实验驱动，探究超对称SU(5)大统一模型中非普适胶子质量情形下的中性微子暗物质太阳信号。

Method: 联合使用micrOMEGAs计算热退耦遗迹丰度，DarkSUSY计算太阳内中性微子捕获率、湮灭截面及中微子/μ子能谱；建立包含局域暗物质丰度、中性微子混合组分（bino/wino/higgsino）及捕获-湮灭平衡条件的信号建模框架。

Result: 揭示了中性微子太阳信号不仅依赖于其在总暗物质中的分数，更显著受其局域密度、弱相互作用组分（尤其是wino/higgsino含量）及是否达捕获-湮灭平衡支配；给出积分通量预言与典型基准点，可直接比对IceCube 79-string与86-string太阳搜索的参考极限。

Conclusion: 本工作构建了一个自洽的理论框架，可用于解释当前太阳中微子实验对次主导中性微子暗物质的灵敏度，并为未来高统计量太阳搜索设定物理可解译的参数空间约束。

Abstract: Motivated by IceCube solar searches, we investigate neutralino dark matter in supersymmetric SU(5) with nonuniversal gaugino masses. Combining micrOMEGAs relic abundances with DarkSUSY calculations, we develop a consistent picture of solar neutrino and muon signals from thermal neutralino subcomponents. We identify how local abundance, neutralino composition, and capture-annihilation equilibrium shape these signals beyond simple scaling with the dark matter fraction. Integrated fluxes and representative benchmarks connect these predictions to published IceCube reference limits. Our results provide a framework for interpreting solar searches and assessing the signals of subdominant neutralino dark matter.

</details>


### [125] [Summary of Aspen discussions on perturbative uncertainties beyond 7-point variations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25091)
*Enzo Canonero,Glen Cowan,Stefano Forte,Joey Huston,Sebastian Jaskiewicz,Manfred Kraus,Christian Preuss,Daniel Reichelt,Peter Skands,Robert Szafron,Yannick Ulrich*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于 Aspen 中心 2024 年暑期研讨会成果，提出在 LHC 高阶散射振幅与事例模拟中，除标准的7点尺度变分外，应补充对已知缺失高阶项的系统性估计，并探讨了在频率学框架下处理非高斯理论不确定性的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升LHC高精度物理分析中理论不确定度的可靠性与完备性，弥合散射振幅计算与实际事例生成之间的鸿沟。

Method: 结合高阶散射振幅理论（如红外结构、软胶子辐射、重求和）与QCD因子化框架，引入额外的缺失高阶项估计方案；并采用频率学统计方法建模非高斯型理论误差分布。

Result: 提出了扩展尺度变分的不确定性估计协议，并给出了至少一种可行的非高斯理论误差量化方案。

Conclusion: 标准的7点尺度变分不足以充分刻画高阶QCD计算中的理论不确定性；需融合振幅层面先验知识与统计建模，以实现更稳健的误差传播。

Abstract: This writeup elaborates on constructive discussions which took place at the Aspen Center for Physics, Aug - Sep 2024, during the program "Tightening the gap between scattering amplitudes and events at the LHC at higher orders". Building on already existing theoretical understanding from the study of scattering amplitudes and resummations at higher orders, we suggest to complement standard 7-point renormalisation- and factorisation-scale variations by additional estimates of missing higher-order terms which are known to be present in the perturbative series. We also summarise a recent proposal for at least one way that non-Gaussian theory uncertainties might be treated within a frequentist framework. This may help to provide more robust and comprehensive uncertainty estimates for experimental and phenomenological applications of perturbative calculations.

</details>


### [126] [Where are the higher excited $B$ and $B_s$ mesons with coupled-channel effects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25125)
*Ru-Hui Ni,Jia-Jun Wu,Xian-Hui Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非淬火夸克模型框架下，研究了B和Bs介子的3S、2P、2D、1F波激发态的质量谱及OZI允许的两体强衰变，发现耦合道效应显著降低质量并改变能级序，尤其影响B(2³P₀)标量态和B(2³D₁)/B(3³S₁)矢量态，并给出了关键实验搜寻道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受新发现的Bs0*(5700)⁰粒子启发，其质量与作者先前对Bs(1³P₀)的非淬火预言高度吻合，从而推动对更高轨道激发态（3S, 2P, 2D, 1F）的系统研究，以检验耦合道效应对重轻介子谱的影响。

Method: 采用非淬火夸克模型（unquenched quark model），即在传统夸克模型中显式引入强耦合道（meson-meson continuum）效应，计算B和Bs介子的高激发态质量谱，并基于^3P₀模型计算OZI允许的两体强衰变宽度与分支比。

Result: 耦合道效应显著降低各激发态质量，尤其使B(2³P₀)质量大幅下移并主导Bπ衰变；B(2³D₁)与B(3³S₁)质量顺序发生反转，较轻的B(2³D₁)主要衰变至B₁(5721)π；多个高激发态（如2³P₂, 2³D₃等）被预言为窄态，可在B^{(*)}π/K、B₁(5721)π/K、B₂*(5747)π/K等末态中寻找。

Conclusion: 耦合道效应在重轻介子高激发谱中起决定性作用，不可忽略；B(2³P₀)和B(2³D₁)是验证该效应的关键候选态；理论预言为LHCb、Belle II等实验提供了明确的搜寻通道与能区指引。

Abstract: Inspired by the newly observed $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$, whose mass agrees well with our previous unquenched prediction for $B_s(1^3P_0)$ [Phys.~Rev.~D \textbf{109}, 116006 (2024)], we study the mass spectra and OZI-allowed two-body strong decays of the higher $3S$-, $2P$-, $2D$-, and $1F$-wave $B$ and $B_s$ mesons in the same unquenched quark model. Our results show that coupled-channel effects significantly lower the masses and modify the relative level spacings. The scalar state $B(2^3P_0)$ is strongly affected: its mass is lowered well below the quenched quark model predictions, and it mainly decays into $B\pi$. Furthermore, the mass ordering of the vector states $B(2^3D_1)$ and $B(3^3S_1)$ is reversed, and the lighter $B(2^3D_1)$ state mainly decays into $B_1(5721)\pi$. To test these results, the $B(2^3P_0)$ and $B(2^3D_1)$ states are worth searching for in the $B\pi$ and $B_1(5721)\pi$ channels, respectively. In addition, some higher excitations are predicted to be relatively narrow and can be searched for in the $B^{(*)}\pi$, $B^{(*)}K$, $B_1(5721)\pi/K$, and $B_2^*(5747)\pi/K$ channels. These predictions provide useful references for future experimental searches and help clarify the role of coupled-channel effects in the heavy-light meson spectrum.

</details>


### [127] [alpha_s from hadron multiplicities: an updated analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25135)
*A.V. Kotikov,B.G. Shaikhatdenov,N.D. Godlevsky,I.A. Kotikov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过在NNLL精度下重求和反常维数，研究了夸克和胶子碎裂函数的首阶Mellin矩D_{q,g}(μ²)，并利用e⁺e⁻对撞实验中带电强子的数据拟合得到αₛ⁽⁵⁾(M_Z) = 0.1177⁺⁰·⁰⁰²⁷₋₀·₀₀₂₅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定强耦合常数αₛ并理解喷注内强子产生机制，特别是夸克与胶子喷注的平均强子多重性差异。

Method: 在NNLL精度下对碎裂函数的反常维数进行重求和，提取首阶Mellin矩D_{q,g}(μ²)，并以e⁺e⁻湮灭实验中带电强子的碎裂数据进行全局拟合。

Result: 拟合结果与实验数据高度一致，并给出五味夸克方案下Z玻色子质量标度处的强耦合常数为αₛ⁽⁵⁾(M_Z) = 0.1177⁺⁰·⁰⁰²⁷₋₀·₀₀₂₅。

Conclusion: NNLL精度下的碎裂函数重求和框架能有效描述喷注强子化过程，所提取的αₛ值具有当前领先精度，支持标准模型QCD预言的一致性。

Abstract: Exploring the anomalous dimensions, resummed through next-to-next-to-leading-logarithmic order, we study the first Mellin moments D_{q,g}(\mu^2) of the quark and gluon fragmentation functions, which correspond to the average hadron multiplicities in jets initiated by quarks and gluons, respectively. By fitting the world data on D_{q,g}(\mu^2) for charged hadrons measured in e^+e^- annihilation, we find a good agreement with the data and obtain the following value for the strong force coupling \alpha_s^{(5)}(M_Z)=0.1177+0.0027-0.0025.

</details>


### [128] [Probing the Energy Evolution and Hadronic Cascade Time Dependence of Forward-Backward Multiplicity Correlations in pp Collisions at $\sqrt{s} = 0.9$--$13.6$~TeV with AMPT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25154)
*Dibakar Dhar,Tumpa Biswas,Prabir Kumar Haldar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用AMPT模型（默认DF和弦熔解SM两种构型）研究了pp碰撞中前向-后向多重数关联（FB correlations），通过系数b_corr系统分析其对赝快度间隙、窗口宽度、质心能量、横动量区间及强子级联时间τ_HC的依赖性，并与ALICE实验数据对比验证。结果表明FB关联主要在部分子阶段形成，强子演化仅轻微稀释该关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能pp碰撞中前向-后向多重数关联的物理起源，区分部分子阶段与强子级联阶段对关联强度的贡献，并检验AMPT模型不同构型（DF vs SM）对实验观测量的描述能力。

Method: 采用AMPT模型的DF和SM配置模拟√s = 0.9–13.6 TeV pp碰撞；计算前向-后向窗口间多重数关联系数b_corr；系统扫描赝快度间隙Δη、方位角窗口宽度Δφ、pT区间及强子级联时间τ_HC（0.6–30 fm/c）；与ALICE测量的dN/dη和π± pT谱交叉校验模型有效性。

Result: b_corr随Δη增大而衰减但保留显著基底；随窗口宽度非线性增长；随√s显著增强；DF构型系统性高于SM构型；SM在低能（0.9, 2.76 TeV）更符合ALICE数据，DF在7 TeV更优；低pT区偏差最大；τ_HC增大导致b_corr单调下降（统计显著但幅度小）。

Conclusion: FB多重数关联强度主要由初始部分子动力学决定，强子级联演化仅起微弱稀释作用；AMPT中弦熔解机制更适于描述低能pp碰撞的长程快度关联。

Abstract: Forward-backward (FB) multiplicity correlations are studied in proton--proton collisions at centre-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} = 0.9$, $2.76$, $5.02$, $7$, $13$, and $13.6$~TeV using the AMPT model in its default (DF) and string melting (SM) configurations. The coefficient $b_{\rm corr}$ is obtained for windows separated in pseudorapidity and azimuth, and its variation with the pseudorapidity gap, window width, collision energy, transverse momentum interval, and hadronic cascade time $\tau_{\rm HC}$ is investigated. The simulated results are compared with the available ALICE measurements, and the model is validated against experimental pseudorapidity densities and charged-pion transverse momentum spectra. The correlation strength is found to decrease gradually with increasing pseudorapidity gap while retaining a substantial pedestal, to rise non-linearly with the window width, and to increase substantially with collision energy. The DF configuration yields systematically larger values of $b_{\rm corr}$ than the SM configuration, while the SM configuration provides the better description of the ALICE data at $0.9$ and $2.76$~TeV and the DF configuration at $7$~TeV. In the lowest transverse momentum intervals the deviations from the data are largest for both configurations. The hadronic cascade time is varied within the SM configuration over $\tau_{\rm HC} = 0.6$, $5$, $15$, and $30$~fm/$c$. A monotonic reduction of $b_{\rm corr}$ with increasing $\tau_{\rm HC}$ is observed at all energies and for all window widths, with the ordering of the curves preserved throughout. The effect is modest but statistically significant. These results indicate that the FB correlation strength is primarily imprinted at the partonic stage and only mildly diluted by the subsequent hadronic evolution.

</details>
