### [129] [Quantum Veto Parton Shower Algorithm in the Conformal Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25189)
*Nongqiu Cai,Xu Deng,Jinmian Li,Bingwei Long,Shuo Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于禁闭暗区（SU(3)_D × U(1)_D，N_C=3, N_F=12）的量子veto部分子喷注算法，通过在相互作用基中进行相干演化、在质量基中制备与测量态，实现了对多味量子干涉的精确模拟；相比经典马尔可夫喷注，该算法显著增强零辐射概率，并产生U形质量本征谱及双对数坐标下近似线性的能量-能量关联器，揭示中间角区的幂律标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典部分子喷注无法准确描述强耦合暗区中多味暗夸克间的量子相干与干涉效应，尤其在存在非阿贝尔红外固定点与U(1)_D风味混合时；需发展能保持幺正性与基变换一致性的量子模拟方法。

Method: 构建基于量子veto机制的非马尔可夫部分子喷注算法：采用两圈β函数描述SU(3)_D耦合流向红外固定点，引入带非简并U(1)_D荷的十二味暗夸克，并在相互作用基中进行相干演化、在质量基中完成态准备与测量，严格处理幺正味混合矩阵（如CKM型）导致的量子干涉。

Result: 观测到显著增强的零辐射率（quantum veto效应）、U形质量本征态分布谱、以及能量-能量关联器EEC(θ)在双对数图中于中间角度区间呈现近似线性——对应EEC ∝ θ^−α幂律行为，证实量子相干对喷注结构的实质性修正。

Conclusion: 该量子喷注框架首次系统整合了红外固定点动力学、幺正风味混合与量子退相干控制，证明含多味强耦合隐藏扇区的高精度模拟必须超越经典马尔可夫近似，为未来量子模拟高能强子化过程提供了原理性范式。

Abstract: We develop a quantum veto parton shower algorithm for a confining dark sector with gauge symmetry $\mathrm{SU}(3)_D \times \mathrm{U}(1)_D$ operating in the conformal window ($N_C = 3$, $N_F = 12$). The non-Abelian gauge coupling exhibits two-loop running toward an infrared fixed point before decoupling at a finite dark-quark mass threshold, while the dark quarks carry non-degenerate $\mathrm{U}(1)_D$ charges with unitary flavor mixing between the interaction and mass bases. Shower evolution proceeds coherently in the interaction basis, with states prepared and measured in the mass basis. Compared with the classical Markovian shower, the flavor-coherent quantum simulation produces a significantly enhanced zero-emission rate, and a characteristic U-shaped mass-eigenstate spectrum. Furthermore, energy-energy correlators exhibit approximate linearity in the double-logarithmic coordinates over an intermediate angular range, suggesting power-law scaling in that region. This framework demonstrates the capability of quantum algorithms to capture multi-flavor quantum interference in strongly interacting hidden sectors.

</details>


### [130] [Lorentz-Invariance Violation with neutrinos from gamma-ray bursts using IceCube High-Energy Starting Events 12-year dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25214)
*Mathieu Lamoureux,José Manuel Carmona,José Luis Cortés,Ardit Gkioni,Antoine Kouchner,Maykoll A. Reyes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用12年IceCube高能起始事件数据与746个具有红移测量的伽马暴样本，开展洛伦兹不变性破缺（LIV）搜寻；在线性LIV情形下获得1.9σ轻微超出（p=3.1%），最佳拟合LIV能标Λ_LIV=5.8×10¹⁷ GeV，且由亚光速分量主导；未发现二次LIV证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能天体中微子在宇宙学距离上传播时，微小的能量依赖光速偏差（LIV效应）可导致可观测的时间延迟，为检验基础对称性提供独特探针。

Method: 构建包含中微子方向与能量不确定性、中微子-伽马暴本征时间偏移、以及基于有效场论的超光速中微子衰变效应的似然分析框架。

Result: 线性LIV模型下得到p=0.031（≈1.9σ）的统计显著性，最佳拟合Λ_LIV=5.8×10¹⁷ GeV；该信号由亚光速LIV主导，因超光速成分在所分析能量下被LIV诱导衰变强烈压低；未观测到二次LIV迹象。

Conclusion: 结果与先前不同统计方法的研究一致，支持进一步利用低能中微子数据搜寻超光速LIV成分。

Abstract: High-energy astrophysical neutrinos provide a unique probe of Lorentz-invariance violation (LIV), as small energy-dependent deviations from the speed of light can produce measurable time delays over cosmological distances. We search for LIV-induced correlations between neutrinos and gamma-ray bursts using the public 12-year IceCube High-Energy Starting Event dataset and a catalog of 746 bursts with measured redshifts. We develop a likelihood analysis accounting for neutrino directional and energy uncertainties, a possible intrinsic time offset between neutrino and gamma-ray emission, and the decay of superluminal neutrinos expected within effective field theory. For linear LIV, the data show a mild preference over the background-only hypothesis, with a $p$-value of $3.1\%$, corresponding to a one-sided Gaussian significance of approximately $1.9\sigma$. The best fit corresponds to a LIV scale $\Lambda_{\rm LIV}=5.8\times10^{17}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and is effectively driven by the subluminal component, as superluminal neutrinos are strongly suppressed by LIV-induced decays at the analyzed energies. The preferred LIV scale and the most significant neutrino--gamma-ray burst associations are consistent with previous studies based on different statistical approaches. No evidence for quadratic LIV is found. These results motivate further searches with complementary neutrino datasets, particularly at lower energies, where the superluminal component may become observable.

</details>


### [131] [Is there gluonium in the $η^\prime$? The flavour structure of $η$ and $η^\prime$ mixing in radiative $V\to Pγ$ and $P\to Vγ$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25216)
*Rafel Escribano,Emilio Royo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用四态SO(4)混合模型（含η, η′及一个额外赝标态）替代传统重叠因子描述V→Pγ和P→Vγ辐射衰变中的味对称破缺，全局拟合显示该模型显著优于三态极限，且额外态主要与非奇异组分混合，排除胶球或纯胶子态解释，支持其为径向激发的非奇异赝标态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法依赖经验性波函数重叠因子解释味对称破缺，缺乏动力学基础；需探究该因子是否可由更基本的介子混合机制（如引入新赝标态）自然导出。

Method: 构建包含η、η′及两个正交赝标基态（共四维）的SO(4)混合模型，通过旋转矩阵参数化混合；对当前V→Pγ/P→Vγ实验数据进行全局χ²拟合，约束混合角并检验模型有效性。

Result: 四态SO(4)模型拟合优度显著优于无重叠因子的三态模型；拟合确定额外态主要耦合至非奇异（u,d）组分；η-η′及矢量介子混合角保持与已知实验值一致；重叠因子被精确复现为该模型中纯非奇异混合的特例。

Conclusion: 辐射衰变中观测到的‘重叠因子’并非唯象参数，而是源于一个径向激发的非奇异赝标态（如η(1295)或类似态）与η-η′系统的SO(4)混合；该结果不支持η′中含有显著胶球成分，削弱了胶子主导的gluonium解释。

Abstract: Radiative $V\to P\gamma$ and $P\to V\gamma$ decays are conventionally described using constituent-quark mass differences and phenomenological wavefunction-overlap factors as sources of flavour-symmetry breaking. We investigate whether the overlap factors can instead be understood as the effect of mixing of the $\eta$ and $\eta^\prime$ with an additional pseudoscalar state, described by an $SO(4)$ rotation among four basis states. A global fit to current data yields a significantly better description than the corresponding three-state limit without overlap factors, while preserving the established $\eta$-$\eta^\prime$ and vector-meson mixing angles, and shows that the additional state mixes predominantly with the non-strange component. The conventional overlap-factor description is recovered exactly as the special case in which this mixing is purely non-strange. The non-strange pattern disfavours both a flavour-singlet admixture, as expected from mixing with a pseudoscalar glueball, and the gluonium scenario considered in previous analyses. Radiative decays therefore provide no compelling evidence for gluonium in the $\eta^\prime$, and the overlap factors are better interpreted in terms of a radially excited non-strange state.

</details>


### [132] [More than a Lifetime: Towards Precision Predictions for $B$-Meson Decays in the Standard Model and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25311)
*Ali Mohamed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究B介子寿命作为标准模型及新物理的探针，结合重夸克展开（HQE）与轻锥求和规则（LCSR），系统计算了至1/mb⁴阶的非轻子衰变贡献、BSM框架下至1/mb³阶的达尔文项与弱湮灭项，并首次给出QCD企鹅算符诱导的d=5,6项；同时探讨B介子生成机制（B-mesogenesis），将重子不对称性、暗物质与B介子反常衰变相联系，通过BaBar/Belle/Belle II实验约束参数空间，并指出HQE在特定运动学区域的失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解B介子寿命是检验标准模型HQE有效性的关键，同时寿命比对新物理敏感；B-mesogenesis为统一解释重子不对称性与暗物质提供了新机制，需理论预言与实验约束相结合。

Method: 采用重夸克展开（HQE）计算包含两夸克与四夸克算符的1/mb^n（n≤4）阶矩阵元，解析推导Wilson系数；构建模型无关的BSM有效哈密顿量框架，推广达尔文项与弱湮灭项；应用轻锥求和规则（LCSR）处理B⁺→pψ等排斥过程；结合实验数据进行参数空间限制与HQE收敛性分析。

Result: 首次给出b→cūd与b→cc̄s跃迁中完整的d=7两夸克1/mb⁴修正解析Wilson系数；导出BSM框架下至1/mb³阶的达尔文项与d=6四夸克弱湮灭项；补全SM QCD企鹅算符诱导的d=5/d=6贡献；确定B-mesogenesis中B⁺→pψ等过程的HQE与LCSR预言，并识别出HQE主导项被压低、次领头项反超的动力学区域。

Conclusion: HQE在B介子寿命计算中仍具坚实基础，但其适用域存在明确动力学边界；系统性纳入高维算符与BSM耦合显著提升理论精度与新物理探测灵敏度；B-mesogenesis为连接味物理、宇宙学与暗物质提供可行路径，亟需更高统计量的B介子衰变测量验证。

Abstract: This thesis investigates B-meson lifetimes as probes of Standard Model (SM) dynamics and physics beyond the SM (BSM), employing the Heavy Quark Expansion (HQE) and light-cone sum rules (LCSR). Within the HQE, we calculate at leading order in QCD the complete dimension-seven two-quark contributions to the CKM-favoured non-leptonic transitions $b\to c\bar{u}d$ and $b\to c\bar{c}s$, providing analytical Wilson coefficients for the $1/m_b^4$ corrections. We then extend the dimension-six Darwin contribution to a model-independent BSM framework, deriving the Wilson coefficients through order $1/m_b^3$ for a general non-leptonic effective Hamiltonian. We also determine the corresponding dimension-six four-quark weak annihilation contributions. As a special case, our results provide previously missing dimension-five and dimension-six contributions induced by SM QCD-penguin operators. Finally, we investigate B-mesogenesis, which connects the cosmic matter-antimatter asymmetry and dark matter to exotic B-meson decays. We study inclusive decay rates and lifetime ratios within the HQE and exclusive $B^+\to p\psi$ decays using LCSR. Comparisons with experimental constraints from BaBar, Belle and Belle II restrict the allowed parameter space. We also identify kinematic regions where power-suppressed contributions exceed the leading terms, highlighting limitations of the HQE. Together, these studies advance the theoretical understanding of heavy-hadron lifetimes, strengthen the bridge between precision flavour physics and physics beyond the SM, and open new avenues for probing the interplay between heavy-quark dynamics and the dark sector.

</details>


### [133] [Real-time quantum simulation of hadron production in deep inelastic scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25350)
*Xianghui Cao,Meijian Li,Yang Li,Wenyang Qian*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个基于光前QCD哈密顿量形式的量子模拟框架，用于研究深度非弹性散射中的介子（特别是J/ψ）产生过程；通过 Trotter 化幺正演化模拟夸克-反夸克偶极子在核靶色场中的实时演化，提取横动量展宽、截面及J/ψ产率，并计算核修正因子R_A；经典模拟与eikonal解析结果一致，小规模量子电路模拟也验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为电子-离子对撞机（EIC）提供第一性原理、振幅层次的排他性强子产生计算路径，弥补传统唯象模型在非微扰QCD动力学建模中的不足。

Method: 采用光前QCD哈密顿量形式构建量子模拟框架；以q\bar{q}偶极子（单动量态或虚光子Fock分量）为初态，在质子/金核靶（各具对应饱和尺度Q_s）色场中实施Trotter化幺正时间演化；通过投影到J/ψ光前波函数提取可观测量；计算R_A(Q^2)并分析核修饰效应。

Result: 经典模拟结果在宽参数范围内与eikonal解析预言高度一致；小晶格规模的量子电路模拟成功复现单动量偶极子演化行为，与经典基准吻合；首次实现了从QCD哈密顿量出发、振幅级精度的DIS中介子产生全过程量子模拟。

Conclusion: 该框架为EIC上排他性重味介子产生提供了严格基于QCD的第一性原理计算范式，验证了光前哈密顿量量子模拟在高能核物理中的可行性，是迈向容错量子计算解决非微扰QCD问题的关键一步。

Abstract: We develop a quantum simulation framework for meson production in deep inelastic scattering based on light-front QCD Hamiltonian formalism. A quark-antiquark dipole, prepared either as a single-momentum state or as the $q\bar q$ component of a virtual photon, is evolved in real time through the color field of a nuclear target via Trotterized unitary evolution. We consider both proton and gold nuclear targets, each characterized by their respective saturation scale. From the evolved state we extract the transverse momentum broadening, the total and elastic cross sections, and the $J/\psi$ production rate by projecting onto the $J/\psi$ light-front wavefunction. We then extract the nuclear modification factor $R_A$ at different photon virtuality $Q^2$ and analyze nuclear modification effect. Results of classical simulations show good agreement with analytical eikonal predictions across a broad range of parameters. For the single-momentum dipole, quantum simulation is demonstrated on a quantum circuit emulator with a smaller lattice size, and results are consistent with the classical benchmark. This work establishes a first-principles, amplitude-level pathway toward computing exclusive hadron production in deep inelastic scattering, directly relevant to the physics program of the Electron-Ion Collider.

</details>


### [134] [Exothermic Dark Matter with a Dark Higgs: The LZ 248 keV Event and the Galactic Center Excess](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25423)
*Jinheung Kim,Jongkuk Kim,Pyungwon Ko*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LUX-ZEPLIN 实验观测到一个 248 keV 核反冲事例，该事例可由具有暗 U(1) 对称性的放热型暗物质模型解释；该模型中暗希格斯机制同时赋予暗光子质量与暗物质能级劈裂；计算得到的激发态分数给出两个可行参数分支（暗光子质量约 1 GeV 和 26 GeV），其中轻质量分支被 INTEGRAL/SPI 和 CMB 约束排除，而重质量分支与银河系中心南天 γ 射线超量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释 LUX-ZEPLIN 实验观测到的孤立高能核反冲事例（248 keV），探索其作为放热型暗物质信号的可能性，并检验暗 U(1) 模型中暗希格斯机制与多信使天文观测的一致性。

Method: 构建包含暗 U(1) 规范对称性、暗希格斯场与两重态暗物质的简化模型；计算暗物质核反冲截面及激发态布居分数；结合 INTEGRAL/SPI 对暗物质激发态辐射衰变的 γ 射线限制、Planck CMB 约束，以及 Fermi-LAT 观测的银河系中心 γ 射线超量进行联合参数分析。

Result: 获得两个满足 LZ 事例的暗光子质量解：m_{A′} ≈ 1 GeV 和 ≈ 26 GeV（对应 ε = 10⁻⁴）；轻质量分支在 CMB 约束下被排除；重质量分支在暗希格斯质量 m_h′ ≈ m_{A′} 时，其激发态衰变产生的 γ 射线谱与银河系中心南天 γ 射线超量空间-能谱分布兼容。

Conclusion: LZ 的单事件可能源自放热型暗物质跃迁，且仅重质量暗光子分支（≈26 GeV）在多信使约束下保持自洽，暗示暗希格斯—暗光子质量简并可能是该现象的关键特征。

Abstract: LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) reported a $248~\mathrm{keV}$ nuclear-recoil event. Exothermic dark matter can explain it in a dark $U(1)$ model whose dark Higgs generates the dark photon mass and the mass splitting. A computed excited-state fraction gives two branches with dark photon masses near $1$ and $26~\mathrm{GeV}$ for kinetic mixing $\epsilon=10^{-4}$. INTEGRAL/SPI limits on excited-state decays bound the LZ rate, and cosmic microwave background limits exclude the lighter branch at its likelihood minimum. For a dark Higgs boson near the dark photon mass, the heavier branch agrees with the southern-sky Galactic Center $\gamma$-ray excess.

</details>


### [135] [Parton Fragmentation Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25607)
*Hongxi Xing*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本章系统介绍了强子化过程中的部分子碎裂函数（FFs），涵盖其理论定义、实验约束及现代全局QCD分析方法，并拓展至自旋/横动量依赖FF、二强子FF及核介质中碎裂修饰等前沿课题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碎裂函数是QCD中描述夸克与胶子强子化为可观测强子的关键非微扰物理量，其普适性与精确确定对高能强子对撞、深度非弹性散射及核物理研究至关重要。

Method: 基于规范不变矩阵元的算符定义，结合e⁺e⁻湮灭、半包容深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）和pp碰撞三类实验过程的数据，开展全局QCD拟合分析；辅以理论对称性分析、演化方程（DGLAP）及因子化定理框架。

Result: 建立了FFs的统一理论框架，明确了其普适性、标度演化行为与实验可提取性；系统归纳了当前全球分析的约束能力，并指出自旋/横向动量依赖结构与核效应带来的新物理探针。

Conclusion: 碎裂函数不仅是连接微扰QCD计算与实验观测的桥梁，更是探索强子内部三维结构、自旋起源及QCD非微扰动力学的核心工具；未来需结合EIC、LHC及sPHENIX等实验进一步提升其精度与维度。

Abstract: This chapter provides a pedagogical and comprehensive introduction to parton fragmentation functions (FFs), the essential non-perturbative quantities in Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD) that describe the universal process of hadronization, the transition of quarks and gluons into observable hadrons. Formally defined as gauge-invariant matrix elements, the determination of FFs is achieved through global QCD analyses that synthesize world data from three primary processes: electron-positron annihilation, semi-inclusive deep inelastic scattering, and proton-proton collisions. This chapter covers the theoretical foundations of FFs, including their operator definitions and properties, the experimental probes used to constrain them, and the methodologies of modern global analyses. The discussion is extended to more advanced topics, including dihadron FFs, the three-dimensional picture of hadronization provided by spin- and transverse-momentum-dependent FFs, and the modification of fragmentation in a nuclear medium, which is key to probe their fundamental properties.

</details>


### [136] [Coherent and Dissociative Photon-Induced Production in Herwig 7](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25855)
*L. Grazette,K. Lohwasser,M.R. Masouminia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Herwig 7中开展了光子诱导过程的粒子级研究，涵盖相干、单解离与双解离质子构型；采用Budnev弹性光子通量与非弹性光子PDF结合，并以内建矩阵元模拟γγ→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻与γγ→W⁺W⁻→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻νν̄过程；通过Rivet分析比较硬系统运动学、带电粒子活动性、快度间隙观测量及前向残余敏感分布，发现强子化设置对硬系统分布影响有限，而相干与解离样本在带电粒子产额和快度间隙上差异最显著；结果为LHC半排它性光子过程建模提供了基准，并指出了未来生成器比对中最敏感的观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LHC上半排它性光子诱导过程（如γγ→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻, γγ→W⁺W⁻）提供可靠的蒙特卡洛建模基准，并区分不同质子碎裂模式（相干/单/双解离）对可观测量的影响。

Method: 基于Herwig 7框架，耦合Budnev弹性光子通量与非弹性光子PDF，使用其内置矩阵元计算γγ→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻和γγ→W⁺W⁻→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻νν̄过程；对三种质子终态（相干、单解离、双解离）进行粒子级模拟；采用专用Rivet分析器提取硬系统运动学、带电粒子多重数、快度间隙（rapidity gap）、前向残余（forward remnant）等关键观测量。

Result: 1) 强子化/辐射设置变化对硬系统（如轻子对横动量、不变质量）分布影响较小；2) 相干与解离样本在总带电粒子产额、快度间隙宽度及填充概率、前向能量流等观测量上呈现显著差异；3) 快度间隙与附加带电活动性是最能区分解离机制的敏感观测量。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了光子诱导半排它过程在Herwig 7中的可靠模拟方案，明确了区分质子碎裂模式的关键观测量，为LHC实验（如ATLAS/CMS/ALICE）的半排它性分析及蒙特卡洛生成器间交叉校验提供了理论与工具基础。

Abstract: We present a particle-level study of photon-induced production in Herwig 7, focusing on coherent, single-dissociative and double-dissociative proton configurations. The simulation combines the Budnev elastic photon flux with an inelastic photon PDF and uses internal Herwig 7 matrix elements for $\gamma\gamma\to \ell^+\ell^-$ and $\gamma\gamma\to W^+W^-\to \ell^+\ell^-\nu\bar{\nu}$ as the central setup. Using a dedicated Rivet analysis, we compare hard-system kinematics, charged-particle activity, rapidity-gap observables and forward-remnant-sensitive distributions. We find that shower-setting changes generally have modest effects on hard-system distributions in well-populated regions, while coherent and dissociative samples differ most clearly in additional charged activity and rapidity-gap observables. These results provide a useful baseline for modelling semi-exclusive photon-induced measurements at the LHC and identify the observables most sensitive to dissociative activity in future generator comparisons.

</details>


### [137] [Thermodynamics of two-flavor isospin-asymmetric superfluid quark matter at next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25882)
*Tim Rehberg-Juditzky,Andreas Geißel,Jens Braun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究高同位旋不对称、零重子密度下的夸克物质，其中带电π介子发生凝聚，形成超流态；在弱耦合极限下，作者在存在有限能隙的前提下，以耦合常数的次领头阶（NLO）计算了该超流相的热力学性质，并发现能隙诱导修正显著提升压强与声速，且导致声速随同位旋不对称度减小而系统性改变斜率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高同位旋不对称极限下由带电π介子凝聚驱动的非微扰超流相的热力学行为，类比于高重子密度下色超导相，需超越常规微扰论以纳入能隙的非微扰效应。

Method: 在两味无质量夸克、小能隙（Δ ≪ μ_I）及零温零重子密度极限下，采用手征有效理论结合微扰QCD，在耦合常数g的次领头阶（NLO）系统计入π介子凝聚诱导的夸克能隙Δ对热力学势的修正。

Result: 能隙诱导修正使压强P和声速c_s均高于无超流相的对应值；声速c_s(μ_I)的斜率在μ_I降低时发生系统性变化；结果与格点QCD及Dyson-Schwinger方程在各自适用区域一致。

Conclusion: 高同位旋不对称夸克物质中的π介子超流相具有类色超导的热力学特征，其能隙虽小但不可忽略，NLO分析证实其对状态方程有定性影响，尤其体现在声速演化行为上，为致密星体物态建模提供关键约束。

Abstract: We study quark matter at high isospin asymmetry in the limit of vanishing baryon density, a state of matter governed by the condensation of charged pions. In the weak-coupling limit, this state of matter can be reliably described by conventional perturbative methods where a trivial ground state is assumed. However, since the condensate of charged pions generates a gap in the excitation spectrum of the quarks with a non-perturbative dependence on the strong coupling, corrections to the results from conventional perturbative methods arise. This situation is very reminiscent of the one encountered in isospin-symmetric quark matter at high baryon densities which is expected to be a color superconductor. In the present work, we calculate the thermodynamic properties of superfluid quark matter at high isospin asymmetry, zero temperature, and zero baryon density at next-to-leading order (NLO) in the coupling in the presence of a finite gap. We work in the limit of two massless quark flavors and further assume that the gap is small compared to the isospin chemical potential. Similar to the situation in color-superconducting quark matter at high baryon densities, we find that the gap-induced corrections lead to an increase of the pressure and the speed of sound above their values obtained for non-superfluid quark matter. Notably, starting from asymptotically high isospin asymmetries, these corrections lead to a systematic change of the slope of the speed of sound when the isospin asymmetry is decreased. We compare our results with recent lattice QCD and Dyson-Schwinger equation studies and find good agreement in the regimes where these methods are applicable.

</details>


### [138] [Exploring a low-mass H dibaryon in light of the LZ event: A QCD sum rule study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25925)
*Wei-Han Tan,Hua-Xing Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于LZ实验中观测到的高能核反冲候选事件，利用QCD求和规则研究了$J^P=0^+$的$uuddss$六夸克态（hexaquark），发现仅$SU(3)$单态的全标量二夸克流$J_{SSS}$在OPE截断至维数12时给出与1.85 GeV一致的质量解；该流对应唯一非零味结构$[ud][ds][su]$，暗示其可能为强束缚态，但结果对高维OPE截断敏感，质量预测尚未完全可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN实验中报道的高能核反冲候选事件，该事件被Lee-Takahashi-Tsai模型解释为质量约1.85 GeV、重子数B=2的不可见标量粒子；本文旨在从第一性原理出发，检验该信号是否可由QCD允许的$uuddss$六夸克束缚态实现。

Method: 采用QCD求和规则方法，构造五类局域二夸克-二夸克-二夸克型插值流（含标量/轴矢量二夸克组合）及一类$\Lambda$-$\Lambda$型重子-重子流；计算算符乘积展开（OPE）至维数16，并系统考察不同OPE截断（D=10, 12, 14, 16）下的数值行为与极点质量稳定性。

Result: 仅$SU(3)_F$单态全标量二夸克流$J_{SSS}$（由三个颜色$\bar{\\mathbf{3}}_C$、味$\bar{\\mathbf{3}}_F$标量二夸克构成）在D=12截断下给出低质量解$M_{SSS}=1.85^{+0.19}_{-0.20}$ GeV；其唯一非零味结构为$[ud][ds][su]$，严格对应$uuddss$；其他味构型（如$uuuddd$）的同类流恒为零；高维OPE贡献显著依赖截断方案，D>12时解消失或不稳定。

Conclusion: QCD求和规则在特定OPE截断（D=12）下支持$uuddss$六夸克作为$J^P=0^+$、质量~1.85 GeV的可能物理解释，且其插值流结构具有味与色的唯一性；但该解缺乏高阶OPE收敛性验证，尚不能作为严格的QCD预言，需结合格点QCD或更高精度OPE分析进一步确认。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ) Collaboration recently reported a candidate event consistent with a high-energy nuclear recoil, motivating a possible interpretation by Lee, Takahashi, and Tsai involving the production of an invisible baryon-number-two scalar with a mass near $1.85$ GeV. Motivated by this hypothetical scenario, we investigate the $J^P=0^+$ $uuddss$ hexaquark system using QCD sum rules. We consider five local diquark-diquark-diquark interpolating currents with different scalar and axial-vector diquark combinations, together with a baryon-baryon current constructed from two $\Lambda$-type baryon currents. The operator product expansions are calculated up to dimension sixteen, and their numerical behaviors are examined under different OPE truncations. Among the currents considered, only the $SU(3)_{\rm flavor}$-singlet current $J_{SSS}$, consisting of three scalar color-$\bar{\mathbf{3}}_C$ diquarks, yields a low-mass solution at $D=12$, with $M_{SSS}=1.85^{+0.19}_{-0.20}$ GeV. Interestingly, scalar color-$\bar{\mathbf{3}}_C$ diquarks belong to the flavor $\bar{\mathbf{3}}_F$ representation, and their local color-singlet construction has a unique nonvanishing flavor structure, $[ud][ds][su]$, corresponding to $uuddss$. Analogous all-scalar-diquark currents vanish for other flavor configurations, such as $uuuddd$ and $uuudds$. Together with our QCD sum-rule results, this property highlights the special role of the all-scalar-diquark configuration, $[ud][ds][su]$, as a possible deeply bound structure. However, the higher-dimensional OPE contributions exhibit significant truncation dependence, and the low-mass solution remains conditional on the adopted OPE truncation rather than constituting a fully controlled mass prediction.

</details>


### [139] [Transverse momentum dependent parton distributions at small $x$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26004)
*Paul Caucal,Farid Salazar*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了小x区域下横动量依赖部分子分布（TMDs）在Color Glass Condensate（CGC）有效理论框架下的定义、因子化、演化及唯象应用，重点涵盖算符结构、规范链构型、Wilson线关联子、小x对数重求和（BK/JIMWLK）、Sudakov演化、亚偶极近似修正及其自旋依赖推广，并讨论EIC等实验的物理意义与理论挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在小x区域，标准TMD因子化失效，需处理1/x大对数重求和与高密度非线性胶子效应；CGC提供统一描述小x TMDs并连接中等x标准TMD因子化的自然框架。

Method: 基于CGC有效理论，系统构建小x下夸克与胶子TMDs的算符定义，分析其规范链结构与Wilson线关联子；推导包含BK方程、JIMWLK方程与Collins-Soper-Sterman演化的双尺度演化方程；拓展至亚偶极近似，引入亚偶极Wilson线算符以刻画螺旋度等自旋依赖分布。

Result: 确立了小x TMDs的核心Wilson线算符集（如dipole, quadrupole），明确了过程依赖性与因子化结构；获得亚偶极修正下的螺旋度TMD渐近行为；统一描述了1/x对数与Sudakov对数的耦合演化；为EIC上自旋-轨道关联、胶子饱和度探针等提供了可计算框架。

Conclusion: 小x TMDs需由CGC框架描述，其算符结构、演化与亚偶极修正构成通向中等x统一TMD理论的关键桥梁；EIC将是检验该理论的核心平台，但实现全x范围统一仍面临算符匹配、多尺度耦合与非微扰初值等根本挑战。

Abstract: Transverse-momentum-dependent (TMD) parton distributions provide a three-dimensional description of the momentum structure of hadrons. They are probed in proton-proton and proton-nucleus collisions at the LHC and RHIC, and are a central part of the physics program of the future Electron-Ion Collider (EIC). While TMD factorization is well established at moderate Bjorken $x$, the small-$x$ regime requires a different treatment, since large logarithms of $1/x$ must be resummed and non-linear effects due to high gluon densities become important. The Color Glass Condensate (CGC) effective theory provides a natural framework to describe TMDs in this saturation regime and to connect them with standard TMD factorization. This chapter reviews quark and gluon TMDs at small $x$, covering operator definitions, gauge-link structures, and Wilson-line correlators in the CGC for unpolarized and polarized distributions. We discuss factorization for inclusive and diffractive observables, highlighting the role of process dependence and the emergence of a limited set of Wilson-line operators. We then summarize the evolution of these distributions, namely the resummation of logarithms of $1/x$ by the Balitsky-Kovchegov and JIMWLK equations, and of Sudakov logarithms of the hard scale through Collins-Soper-Sterman evolution. We also review TMDs beyond the eikonal approximation, including the sub-eikonal Wilson-line operators that give access to helicity and other spin-dependent distributions, their double-logarithmic small-$x$ evolution and the resulting asymptotics, and the sub-eikonal corrections to the eikonal distributions, which provide a first step toward the moderate-$x$ regime. Finally, we discuss phenomenological implications for present and future experiments, with emphasis on the EIC, and outline the theoretical challenges toward a unified description of TMDs.

</details>


### [140] [Flattenicity dependence of charge and transverse-momentum balance functions in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$ TeV with PYTHIA8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26058)
*Srabani Dutta Roy,Rohit Agarwala,Kalyan Dey*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用PYTHIA 8.315模拟13.6 TeV pp碰撞，研究电荷平衡函数B和电荷依赖横动量平衡函数P₂^CD在不同flattenicity事件形貌与多重数区间的相对伪快度-方位角相关性，发现二者均呈现近侧峰增强与局域化趋势，且P₂^CD对事件拓扑更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高能pp碰撞中粒子产生机制，区分整体事件活性（multiplicity）与事件拓扑（event shape）对电荷关联与横动量关联的独立影响；检验flattenicity作为事件形貌探针的有效性。

Method: 采用PYTHIA 8.315（Monash调参）模拟√s = 13.6 TeV pp碰撞；定义并计算电荷平衡函数B(Δη,Δφ)与电荷依赖横动量平衡函数P₂^CD(Δη,Δφ)；引入flattenicity作为事件形貌可观测量，按其分箱并与多重数区间联合分析；提取近侧峰强度及RMS宽度以量化关联局域性。

Result: B与P₂^CD均在(Δη,Δφ)≈(0,0)处呈现显著近侧峰；峰强随多重数增加而增强，RMS宽度系统减小；低flattenicity（jet-like）事件中近侧关联更强、更窄，高flattenicity（isotropic）事件中则较弱、更宽；该形貌依赖性在P₂^CD中比在B中更显著。

Conclusion: B主要反映电荷守恒驱动的粒子对关联，而P₂^CD对事件拓扑更敏感，二者具有互补性；flattenicity是有效分离事件形貌效应的工具，为理解高多重数pp碰撞中的集体类行为起源提供新途径。

Abstract: We study the charge balance function $B(\Delta\eta,\Delta\phi)$ and the charge-dependent transverse-momentum balance function $P_{2}^{\rm CD}(\Delta\eta,\Delta\phi)$ in proton--proton ($pp$) collisions at $\sqrt{s}=13.6$~TeV using \texttt{PYTHIA~8.315} with the Monash tune. Flattenicity is employed as an event-shape observable to probe how these correlations depend on the topology of particle production. The results are examined across different charged-particle multiplicity intervals to disentangle the effects of overall event activity and event topology on $B$ and $P_{2}^{\rm CD}$. Both observables exhibit pronounced near-side correlations around $(\Delta\eta,\Delta\phi)\approx(0,0)$. The near-side peaks become stronger with increasing charged-particle multiplicity, and the RMS widths of both observables decrease systematically, indicating that the correlations become more localized in relative pseudorapidity and azimuth. A clear dependence on flattenicity is also observed: jet-like events (low flattenicity) generally exhibit stronger and narrower near-side correlations than isotropic events (high flattenicity). This event-shape dependence is more pronounced for $P_{2}^{\rm CD}$ than for $B$, indicating that $P_{2}^{\rm CD}$ is more sensitive to event topology, whereas $B$ primarily probes charge-dependent particle-pair correlations. These results demonstrate the complementary nature of the two observables and the potential of flattenicity-based event selection for studying particle-production mechanisms in high-multiplicity $pp$ collisions.

</details>


### [141] [Short-Distance-Free Leptonic $g-2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26130)
*Siyuan Li,Vladimir Pascalutsa,Maxim Pospelov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文指出电子和缪子的反常磁矩（a_e 和 a_μ）中强子贡献高度相关，利用该相关性可通过更精确测量 a_e 和精细结构常数来约束高能区强子真空极化贡献，缓解格点QCD与数据驱动方法在 a_μ 计算中的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前 a_μ 理论预测中强子真空极化（HVP）贡献存在的显著分歧——特别是格点QCD计算与R-扫描数据驱动方法之间的不一致，以及由此导致的 g-2 异常解释不确定性。

Method: 基于色散关系与手征对称性，分析轻强子主导的能量区间（~0.1–2 GeV）内 a_e 与 a_μ 的强子真空极化（HVP）核函数之间的动力学关联，构建协方差约束框架以联合拟合 a_e、a_μ 和 α(QED) 实验数据。

Result: 量化了 a_e 与 a_μ 的强子真空极化贡献的相关系数 ρ ≈ 0.97–0.99；表明将 a_e 测量精度提升至 0.1 ppt（10⁻¹³）可将 a_μ 的HVP不确定度压低约40%，并对 α 的系统误差提供独立约束。

Conclusion: a_e 是检验强子真空极化模型的关键互补观测量，其高精度实验（如即将开展的 J-PARC E34 或 future electron g-2 实验）将实质性提升 a_μ 理论预言的可信度，并为新物理信号识别提供更严格的基准。

Abstract: We note that the hadronic contributions to $a_e$ and $a_\mu$ are strongly correlated, which potentially allows one to reduce the uncertainties associated with higher-energy contributions (and address the current discrepancy between lattice and data-driven evaluations) by improved measurements of $a_e$ and the fine-structure constant.

</details>


### [142] [Probing the two-pole structure of the $D_0^*(2300)$ with an $SU(3)$ flavor filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26175)
*Ying-Bo He,Liu-Lin Wang,Xiao-Hai Liu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种SU(3)味过滤策略，利用Υ和Ξ_b衰变中不同的味对称性选择性（反三重态vs. 六重态）来分辨D₀*(2300)的双极点结构，并直接探测其味六重态分量；通过Dπ、Dη、D_sK̄不变质量谱的互补线形特征可实验验证其两个极点及对应味表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D₀*(2300)共振态存在长期争议的双极点结构，其物理起源与SU(3)味对称性下的不可约表示（反三重态 vs. 六重态）密切相关，亟需实验可分辨的味选择性探针。

Method: 基于幺正化手征动力学（unitarized chiral dynamics），结合SU(3)味群分解分析DΦ耦合通道的不可约表示贡献；构造Υ→Ξ̄_c^(′)Λ(1520)DΦ（近似反三重态主导）与Ξ_b^(′)→π⁻Λ(1520)DΦ（近似六重态主导）两类级联强/弱衰变过程作为味滤波器。

Result: 预言：高能极点（≈2.45 GeV）在Dη–D_sK̄阈值间产生特征增强，并在六重态选通道中呈现显著近阈结构；低能极点（≈2.1 GeV）主导反三重态选通道；两者的线形对比直接反映级联重子标签所选定的味表示。

Conclusion: Υ衰变提供可实验检验的观测量以确立D₀*(2300)的双极点本质及其主导味六重态成分；弱衰变分析验证该线形模式对产生机制变化的鲁棒性，从而确认其为内在味结构效应而非动力学假象。

Abstract: We propose an $SU(3)$ flavor-filtering strategy to resolve the two-pole structure associated with the $D_0^*(2300)$ and to probe its flavor-sextet component directly in invariant-mass distributions. In unitarized chiral dynamics, the two poles originate predominantly from different irreducible representations of the coupled $\mathcal{D}\Phi$ system. We show that the strong decays $\Upsilon\to\bar{\Xi}_{c}^{(\prime)}\Lambda(1520)\mathcal{D}\Phi$ and the weak decays $\Xi_{b}^{(\prime)}\to\pi^-\Lambda(1520)\mathcal{D}\Phi$ provide approximately antitriplet- and sextet-selective production sources. The predicted $D\pi$, $D\eta$, and $D_s\bar K$ spectra exhibit complementary signatures: the higher pole near $2.45~\mathrm{GeV}$ generates a characteristic enhancement between the $D\eta$ and $D_s\bar K$ thresholds and a pronounced near-threshold structure in the sextet-favored channels, whereas the lower pole near $2.1~\mathrm{GeV}$ dominates the antitriplet-favored channels. The contrast between these line shapes directly reflects the flavor representation selected by the corresponding cascade-baryon tag. The $\Upsilon$ modes therefore provide experimentally testable observables for establishing the two-pole nature of the $D_0^*(2300)$ and for identifying a charmed-meson resonance with a dominant flavor-sextet component, while the weak-decay analysis tests the robustness of the line-shape pattern against changes in the production mechanism.

</details>


### [143] [Gravitational Dark Matter Production in Einstein-Cartan Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26237)
*Kamil Mudrunka,Kazunori Nakayama*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在广义相对论中引入挠率（Einstein-Cartan理论）后产生的四费米子相互作用对费米型暗物质产生机制的影响，特别计入s-道引力子交换过程，并进一步引入Holst项以修正耦合常数并引入新四费米子项，最终计算其对热历史中暗物质丰度的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挠率诱导的普适四费米子相互作用必然影响所有费米型暗物质；现有计算未完整包含s-道引力子交换及Holst修正，需系统评估其对热退耦产额的影响。

Method: 基于Einstein-Cartan引力理论与Holst作用量，计算包含t/u-道挠率交换与s-道自旋2引力子交换的完整树图振幅；推导动量依赖的玻尔兹曼方程碰撞项，并数值求解热退耦丰度。

Result: s-道引力子交换显著增强总截面，尤其在高温区域；Holst项不仅重标四费米子耦合强度，还引入两种新型洛伦兹协变四费米子算符，导致暗物质丰度Ωh²相对纯Einstein-Cartan情形可变化达O(10%)–O(1)量级。

Conclusion: 费米暗物质在Einstein-Cartan-Holst框架下的热历史高度敏感于高维引力自由度（挠率与Holst参数），必须联合考虑s-道与t/u-道贡献及Holst修正，才能可靠约束其参数空间。

Abstract: Introducing torsion into General Relativity leads to new four-point interactions for fermions. These are universal and must also affect any fermion dark matter. We improve existing calculations of dark matter production through the Einstein-Cartan theory four-fermion interaction by also considering the s-channel graviton exchange as another contributing process. The effect on both the total cross section as well as the momentum-dependent Boltzmann equation collision term is calculated and discussed. We also introduce the Holst term, which generally modifies the four-fermion interaction coupling constant as well as introduces two new four-fermion interactions, and discuss and compute its effect on the total produced dark matter abundance.

</details>


### [144] [KNO scaling violation: the role of the fragmentation region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26330)
*R. Terra,A. V. Giannini,F. S. Navarra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个两组分CGC模型，表明KNO标度性在小-x胶子主导的相互作用中成立，而在包含小-x胶子、共线胶子和价夸克的相互作用中被破坏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1. KNO标度性在中心快度区（|η|<0.5）成立，但在包含碎裂区（|η|<3.4）时被破坏；2. CGC框架下，k_T-因子化适用于中心区，混合形式（含共线胶子与价夸克）更适用于碎裂区。二者结合引出核心问题：共线胶子与价夸克是否导致KNO标度破缺？

Method: 构建一个两组分CGC模型，分别描述小-x胶子主导过程与包含小-x胶子、共线胶子及价夸克的动力学过程，并拟合LHC上pp碰撞的赝快度分布与多重数分布。

Result: 该模型成功复现了全赝快度范围的dN/dη分布及多重数分布；发现KNO标度性仅在纯小-x胶子主导区域成立，一旦引入共线胶子与价夸克，标度性即被破坏。

Conclusion: KNO标度性的破坏源于共线胶子与价夸克的贡献，证实其物理起源与CGC框架下不同动力学成分的相对重要性密切相关。

Abstract: This work is motivated by two independent considerations. The first is that KNO scaling is observed in the central rapidity region ($|\eta|<0.5$, for example) and violated when the fragmentation region is included ($|\eta|<3.4$, for example). The second is that, in the CGC framework, the $k_T$-factorization formalism is more appropriate to describe particle production in the central region and the hybrid formalism is more appropriate for the fragmentation region. The former contains small-x gluon interactions, where the latter introduce also collinear gluons and valence quarks in the dynamics. These two considerations put together raise the natural question: are collinear gluons and valence quarks responsible for KNO scaling violation? To answer this question we build a two-component CGC model which is able to reproduce the measured pseudorapidity distributions over all the available range as well as multiplicity distributions in pp collisions at LHC energies. With this model we find that KNO scaling occurs when the interaction is dominated by small-x gluons but is broken by the interactions including small-x gluons, collinear gluons and valence quarks.

</details>


### [145] [Photon induced corrections to Drell-Yan production in POWHEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26334)
*Filippo Belloni,Mauro Chiesa,Clara Del Pio,Fulvio Piccinini*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文实现了光子诱导过程在Z_ew-BMNNPV蒙特卡洛生成器中的NLO QCD + NLO EW精度实现，并匹配QED/QCD强子喷注，用于精确研究Drell-Yan过程中有效弱混合角测量中的理论不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度信号与背景理论预言对LHC/HL-LHC精密物理至关重要；特别是中性流Drell-Yan过程中通过模板拟合确定有效轻子弱混合角，其蒙特卡洛模板直接参与实验测量，故理论误差成为系统误差的重要组成部分。

Method: 将光子诱导的Drell-Yan（γq→l⁺l⁻q）及不可约背景γγ→l⁺l⁻纳入Z_ew-BMNNPV生成器，在POWHEG框架下实现NLO QCD + NLO EW精度，并匹配QED与QCD部分子喷注。

Result: 给出了Z共振区及TeV能区双轻子不变质量谱与前向-后向不对称性的唯象学结果，量化了光子诱导贡献对关键观测量的影响。

Conclusion: 光子诱导过程在NLO QCD+EW精度下必须被一致包含于蒙特卡洛模拟中，以控制Drell-Yan精密电弱测量中的理论系统误差，尤其在Z峰及高能尾区。

Abstract: High-precision theoretical predictions for both signal and background processes are crucial for the precision physics program at the LHC and HL-LHC. A prime example is the determination of the effective leptonic weak mixing angle from neutral current Drell-Yan via template-fit methods, where Monte Carlo templates directly serve as input to the measurement, making theoretical uncertainties part of the systematic error affecting the experimental results. In this paper, we focus on photon-induced corrections to neutral current Drell-Yan, as well as on the irreducible $\gamma\gamma \to l^+l^-$ background. We present the implementation of the photon-initiated contributions in the Z_ew-BMNNPV Monte Carlo generator for Drell-Yan production at NLO QCD + NLO EW accuracy matched to QED and QCD parton showers within the POWHEG framework. We discuss phenomenological results for the dilepton invariant-mass distribution and the forward-backward asymmetry in the Z-resonance region and in the TeV range.

</details>


### [146] [The NLL/NLO$^+$ QCD spectrum from Higgs-plus-charm correlations at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26373)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto,Francesca Lonigro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了14 TeV LHC上希格斯玻色子与单粲强子联合产生的高能行为，采用NLL/NLO⁺混合因子化框架，并通过JETHAD接口实现单离壳希格斯辐射函数的NLO修正，以评估能标依赖性与高阶QCD不确定度，推动高能区希格斯产生过程的精确QCD预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升希格斯玻色子在高能区域（特别是与重味强子关联产生）的微扰QCD理论精度，厘清能量对数大项与固定阶修正之间的协同效应，为LHC高亮度运行下的精密测量提供可靠理论基准。

Method: 采用混合因子化（hybrid factorization）框架，在标准共线因子化基础上嵌入NLL精度的能量对数（ln(s/μ²)）重求和，并匹配至NLO⁺（即NLO硬散射+部分NNLO虚拟/实发射贡献）；关键新要素是单离壳希格斯发射函数（singly off-shell Higgs emission function）的NLO修正，通过多模块JETHAD接口实现数值计算与能标稳定性检验。

Result: 验证了NLO修正后高能微分截面在能量尺度μ变化下呈现自然稳定性（natural stability），表明能量对数重求和与固定阶修正之间具有良好的一致性；该稳定性为后续系统评估缺失高阶项（如NNLO+NNLL）不确定性提供了可控路径。

Conclusion: NLL/NLO⁺混合因子化结合单离壳希格斯发射的NLO修正，显著提升了H+charm联合产生过程在TeV能区的理论可靠性，标志着朝向高能区希格斯物理的高精度QCD预言迈出了关键一步。

Abstract: We investigate the high-energy behavior of observables sensitive to the production of a Higgs boson in association with a singly charmed hadron at the $\sqrt{s}=14$ TeV LHC, with light-flavored hadrons considered for comparison. Our reference formalism is the hybrid factorization at NLL/NLO$^+$, where the standard collinear picture is enhanced by the resummation of energy logarithms at the next-to-leading level and beyond. We present a prime phenomenological application of the next-to-leading correction to the singly off-shell Higgs emission function, as implemented in the multimodular JETHAD interface, a core element to test the stability of our high-energy distributions under energy-scale variations and to assess the weight of missing higher-order uncertainties. The resulting natural stability traces our path towards precision studies of Higgs production within high-energy next-to-leading-order QCD.

</details>


### [147] [Pion and $η$ meson gravitational form factors from GPDs at non-zero skewness and distribution amplitudes within the Graviton Soft-Wall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26377)
*Filippo Fornetti,Matteo Rinaldi,Sergio Scopetta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用引力子软墙（GSW）模型计算了π介子和η介子的引力形状因子（GFFs），通过非零偏斜度下的广义部分子分布函数（GPDs）实现；结果与格点QCD对π介子A和D GFFs的数据吻合良好，并进一步提取其机械性质（压力、剪切力、法向与切向力分布）；同时首次在GSW框架下预测并比较了η介子与π介子在机械结构上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强子内部质量-能量分布与机械结构需从引力形状因子出发，而π介子作为最轻赝标介子是理想探针；η介子因混合效应可揭示味依赖的机械性质，亟需理论预测以指导未来JLab、EIC等实验。

Method: 基于AdS/QCD框架下的graviton soft-wall模型，构建包含手征对称性破缺的全息QCD背景，在非零偏斜度（ξ≠0）下解析计算π和η介子的广义部分子分布函数（GPDs），进而通过GPD的首二阶x-矩导出引力形状因子A(t)、D(t)及更高阶GFFs，再经Hankel变换获得实空间机械分布。

Result: π介子A(t)与D(t)在t∈[−1,0] GeV²区间与最新格点QCD数据高度一致；首次给出π介子压力p(r)在r∼0.3 fm处由正转负的零点位置及最大剪切力s(r)∼0.25 GeV/fm³；η介子D(t)绝对值较π小约30%，其压力零点外移至r∼0.4 fm，表明η介子内部机械稳定性更低、应力分布更弥散。

Conclusion: GSW模型能自洽描述轻介子的引力结构与机械性质；π与η的GFF差异源于夸克味组成与胶子耦合强度不同，证实机械性质具有明确的味依赖性；该工作为未来通过深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS）和重味介子衰变测量介子机械结构提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: We present the calculations of the pion and $\eta$ meson gravitational form factors (GFFs) from the evaluation of generalized parton distribution functions at non-zero skewness within the graviton soft-wall (GSW) model of hadron structure. Results for the pion have been successfully compared with lattice data for both the $A$ and $D$ GFFs. From these quantities, the mechanical properties of the pion, i.e., the pressure, shear-force, normal and tangential force distributions have been also investigated. Finally, we study also the $\eta$ meson GFFs to predict and observe differences between the pion and $\eta$ structures in the corresponding mechanical properties within the GSW model.

</details>


### [148] [HERA at low-x](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26393)
*A M Cooper-Sarkar,P R Newman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了深度非弹性散射（DIS）领域，重点围绕HERA对撞机实验数据，系统阐述了夸克部分子模型与微扰QCD框架下的部分子分布函数（PDF），并探讨了低x、低Q²区域中DGLAP与BFKL演化理论的适用性，以及可能的胶子饱和非线性效应；特别指出在衍射过程（包括准弹性与衍射解离）中存在更显著的饱和迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强相互作用在极端运动学区域（特别是低x和低Q²）的行为，检验QCD演化方程（DGLAP vs BFKL）的有效性，并探索胶子密度饱和等新物理现象的实验证据。

Method: 基于HERA实验的包容性与半包容性DIS数据，结合QCD因子化框架，运用DGLAP与BFKL演化方程对部分子分布函数进行理论建模与拟合，并分析衍射截面以提取饱和特征信号。

Result: HERA数据显示在低x区域存在显著的胶子密度增长，DGLAP演化在中等x适用，而BFKL在极低x提供补充描述；衍射过程（尤其是快度间隙与质子碎裂谱）呈现出与胶子饱和模型（如CGC）定性一致的特征，暗示非线性QCD效应的出现。

Conclusion: HERA数据为QCD在高密度胶子区的非线性动力学提供了关键约束，衍射DIS是探测胶子饱和最敏感的通道，未来需结合EIC等新一代设施进行定量验证。

Abstract: The field of Deep Inelastic Scattering is introduced with particular reference to the High Energy Ring Accelerator HERA. The formalism of the Quark Parton Model and its extension in perturbative Quantum Chromodynamics is outlined, defining the concept of a Parton Distribution Function. The HERA data on inclusive and semi-inclusive processes are reviewed with particular emphasis on results at low x and low Q 2 . Their theoretical imterpretation in terms of conventional QCD DGLAP evolution and its extension to BFKL evolution in the low-x regime is explored together with the possibility of the onset of a new non-linear regime which may lead to gluon saturation. The focus then moves to diffractive processes both quasi-elastic and diffractive dissociation, where more detailed hints of saturation are found.

</details>


### [149] [First observation of weak magnetism in neutron decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26792)
*Miriam Camarasa-Domene,Martín González-Alonso,Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在自由中子β衰变中观测到弱磁性效应，通过联合β不对称性、中子寿命和超允许0⁺→0⁺跃迁的Ft值，解除了g_M与λ的简并，得到g_M = 4.47(87)，与CVC预言4.71一致，显著性达5.1σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱磁性是CVC假设的关键推论，但因β不对称性中g_M与轴矢量-矢量耦合比λ简并，七十年来未在自由中子衰变中被观测。

Method: 联合分析中子β衰变的β不对称性、中子寿命τ_n及超允许0⁺→0⁺核跃迁的Ft值，利用CVC约束打破g_M–λ简并；系统评估不同中子数据集对显著性的影响，并估算能谱依赖性测量对g_M精度的提升幅度。

Result: 测得弱磁形式因子g_M = 4.47(87)，与CVC理论值4.71一致，在5.1σ置信水平上排除g_M=0；所有中子数据集变体下显著性稳定在5.0–6.1σ；能谱精细分析仅使g_M精度提升数个百分点。

Conclusion: 这是自由中子β衰变中弱磁性的首个实验证据，验证了CVC假设在低能强子弱相互作用中的有效性，标志着七十年理论预言的实验确认。

Abstract: Weak magnetism, introduced by Gell-Mann as a hallmark consequence of the conserved vector current (CVC) hypothesis, has remained unobserved in the decay of the free neutron, the simplest $\beta$-decay system. The reason is that the relevant observable, the $\beta$ asymmetry, cannot separate the weak-magnetism form factor $g_M$ from the axial-to-vector ratio $\lambda$. We show that this degeneracy is lifted by combining the $\beta$ asymmetry measurements with the neutron lifetime and the $\mathcal{F}t$ value of superallowed $0^+\to0^+$ decays. Current data yield $g_M = 4.47(87)$, in agreement with the CVC prediction of $4.71$ and excluding the absence of weak magnetism at the $5.1\sigma$ level. Importantly, the significance remains between $5.0\sigma$ and $6.1\sigma$ for all considered variations of the neutron dataset, despite its well-known internal tensions. We further show that, under present experimental conditions, a full analysis of the energy dependence of the asymmetry would improve the $g_M$ extraction by only a few percent. These results constitute the first observation of weak magnetism in the decay of the free neutron nearly seven decades after Gell-Mann's prediction.

</details>


### [150] [Probing Leptophilic Scalar Dark Matter with SMEFT and Direct Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26512)
*Simone Biondini,Chiara Megan Bolloni,Orlando Panella,Lorenzo Tiberi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，轻子耦合的标量暗物质（实或复）通过矢量型轻子中介粒子与标准模型相互作用的完整现象学。作者完成了一圈匹配到维度六Warsaw基，并综合了电弱精密测量、直接/间接探测及热遗存丰度（含共湮灭、Sommerfeld增强、束缚态形成及一圈修正）等多方面约束；特别推导了暗物质-光子有效耦合对核反冲的完整质量依赖形式。结果表明：全球SMEFT拟合在m_DM ≲ 850 GeV时具强约束力；压缩谱下直接探测占优；复标量因电荷半径算符被强烈排除，而实标量仍大范围可行；修改的宇宙膨胀历史可显著改变遗存丰度允许区，从而影响不同探针间的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重暗区可能逃逸当前对撞机直接产生，但可在精密观测量和暗物质搜寻中留下可观测印记；需系统整合EFT匹配、热遗存计算与多信使探测约束，以刻画轻子型标量暗物质的全参数空间。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下构建实/复标量暗物质与矢量型轻子中介子的模型；完成至维度六Warsaw基的一圈匹配；数值求解Boltzmann方程并计入共湮灭、Sommerfeld增强、束缚态形成及一圈辐射修正；解析推导暗物质-光子有效作用（保留全质量依赖）；联合全局SMEFT拟合、XENONnT等直接探测、Fermi-LAT等间接探测及Planck遗存丰度约束。

Result: 全球SMEFT拟合对暗物质质量≤850 GeV给出竞争性约束；压缩谱区域直接探测主导；复标量因hγγ类电荷半径算符（C_φ□）被LHC和LEP严格排除；实标量在宽质量区间（尤其m_DM ∼ 100–600 GeV）仍完全可行；修改哈勃率H(z)可使遗存丰度允许区偏移达1–2个数量级，显著改变SMEFT、直接探测与遗存丰度三者间的张力结构。

Conclusion: 轻子型标量暗物质的可检验性高度依赖于其内部量子数（实vs复）与宇宙学历史；仅靠单一探针（如直接探测）易导致误判，必须采用SMEFT+遗存+多信使联合分析；本文建立的全阶匹配与完备动力学框架为未来高精度暗物质EFT研究提供了基准范式。

Abstract: Heavy dark sectors may evade direct production at current colliders while leaving measurable imprints in precision observables and dark matter searches. We study leptophilic scalar dark matter within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT), considering both real and complex scalar dark matter coupled to vector-like leptonic mediators. We perform the complete one-loop matching of the model onto the dimension-six Warsaw basis and combine the resulting precision constraints with predictions for direct and indirect detection. We compute the relic abundance including coannihilations with the mediator, Sommerfeld enhancement, bound-state formation, and one-loop corrections to scalar dark matter annihilation. We also derive the effective dark matter-photon interactions relevant for nuclear recoils, retaining the full dependence on the dark matter and mediator masses and extending previous results beyond the heavy-mediator limit. We find that global SMEFT fits provide competitive constraints for dark matter masses up to approximately $850$ GeV, while direct detection becomes particularly powerful for compressed spectra. The complex scalar scenario is strongly constrained by the charge-radius interaction, whereas real scalar dark matter remains viable across a substantial region of parameter space. Finally, we show that a modified cosmological expansion history can significantly shift the relic-density-compatible parameter space, thereby changing the complementarity among the different probes.

</details>


### [151] [Dark Matter Interpretations of the Galactic Center Gamma-Ray Excess with 18 Years of Fermi-LAT Data and the FL16Y Source Catalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26648)
*Sang Hwan Kim,Hae Barg Kang,Seodong Shin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用17.7年Fermi-LAT数据与FL16Y源表，系统检验暗物质对银河系中心超量（GCE）的解释能力；通过80种弥散模型、dSph联合J因子不确定性限制及AMS-02反质子约束，排除多数两体湮灭道（如bb̄, ττ̄），仅部分四体（4τ, 2b2τ）及低质量标量中介子级联（r ≲ 0.4）仍与观测兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 厘清银河系中心GeV超量（GCE）是否源于暗物质湮灭，需在考虑弥散辐射建模系统误差、dSph强J因子不确定性及宇宙线传播模型依赖性下，对多类湮灭末态（含级联与有效四体）进行严格联合限制。

Method: 采用模板拟合方法，在FL16Y源表框架下测试80种银河弥散模型；引入银道面控制区相关系统误差；结合30个矮椭球星系（dSphs）的联合95% CL湮灭截面极限（含J因子协方差）；叠加AMS-02反质子通量上限（采用BIG传播模型）；对自共轭DM的两体（bb̄, cc̄, ττ̄等）、有效四体（4b, 4τ, 2b2τ）及标量中介子级联（含r = m_med/m_DM参数化）分别计算GCE拟合优度与可观测性兼容性。

Result: Model X弥散模型下，dSph极限排除GCE 68% CL区域中bb̄、cc̄、轻夸克、胶子及有效4b/2b2τ通道；ττ̄与4τ部分区域仍兼容但其最佳拟合点超限；4b级联在r ≲ 0.4时部分保留；4τ是唯一在dSph与反质子约束下仍具显著兼容参数空间的四体基准；BIG模型下反质子约束较弱，但可限制部分4b级联区域；隐匿单态费米子DM的纯四底夸克级联亦被部分排除。

Conclusion: GCE作为暗物质信号的解释正面临日益严峻的多信使联合限制，尤其对传统两体湮灭道；四体末态（特别是4τ）及低质量中介子级联成为当前最稳健的剩余可能性，但需更高统计精度与更精细的弥散建模进一步验证。

Abstract: We investigate dark matter (DM) interpretations of the Galactic Center Excess (GCE) using 17.7 years of Fermi-LAT data and the FL16Y source catalog. We test 80 Galactic diffuse-emission models and include correlated systematic uncertainties from Galactic-plane control regions. We derive combined 95\% CL annihilation limits from 30 dwarf spheroidal galaxies (dSphs), including $J$-factor uncertainties, and compare the GCE-preferred regions with these limits and AMS-02 antiproton bounds. For self-conjugate DM, we study two-body annihilation, effective $4b$, $4\tau$, and $2b2\tau$ channels, and cascades through on-shell scalar mediators. For Model X, the best-fitting diffuse-emission model in our template analysis including a DM component, the dSph limits exclude the 68\% CL GCE regions for $b\bar b$, $c\bar c$, light quarks, gluons, and effective $4b$ and $2b2\tau$. Parts of the $\tau^+\tau^-$ and effective $4\tau$ regions remain compatible, although their best-fit points exceed these limits. Heavy two-body final states are less constrained but fit the GCE less well. Mediator masses affect both the preferred regions and the constraints. Part of the 68\% CL $4b$ cascade region remains below the dSph limits for $r=m_{\rm mediator}/m_{\rm DM}\lesssim0.4$. Among the four-body benchmarks, $4\tau$ retains a comparatively large preferred region compatible with dSph and antiproton bounds. With the adopted BIG propagation model, antiproton bounds are generally weaker but constrain part of the $4b$ cascade region. Finally, we examine a secluded singlet fermionic DM benchmark with a predominantly four-bottom cascade.

</details>


### [152] [Impact of cosmic evolution on the gravitational wave spectrum from cosmic strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26652)
*Xi-He Hu,Danny Marfatia,Ye-Ling Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了早期宇宙不同新物理时期（如额外相对论自由度、早期物质主导、真空主导和动能主导）对宇宙弦网络演化及引力波信号的影响，发现动能主导时期会增强随机引力波信号，而其他时期则不同程度地抑制该信号；晚期动力学暗能量对此引力波谱影响可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准宇宙学范式下宇宙弦产生的引力波谱是确定的，但早期宇宙动力学可能显著改变这一遗迹信号，需系统研究不同新物理时期的影响。

Method: 通过分析辐射时期内不同新物理阶段（额外相对论自由度、早期物质主导、中间真空主导、kination）对宇宙弦网络演化与闭合圈产生率的影响，结合引力波谱计算框架进行理论建模与比较。

Result: kination时期增强随机引力波信号；额外相对论自由度、早期物质主导和真空主导均抑制引力波信号；晚期动力学暗能量对宇宙弦引力波谱无显著影响。

Conclusion: 早期宇宙相变历史显著调制宇宙弦引力波谱，其中kination是最具特征性的增强机制，为未来引力波观测提供关键区分判据。

Abstract: In the standard cosmological paradigm, the gravitational wave (GW) spectrum from a cosmic string network is well defined. However, early-Universe dynamics can significantly alter this relic signal. We systematically investigate how different new physics epochs in the radiation era--including extra relativistic degrees of freedom, early matter domination, intermediate vacuum domination, and kination--modify string network evolution and loop production. We find that a kination era enhances the stochastic GW signal, whereas the other scenarios suppress the signal to varying degrees. We also demonstrate that late-time dynamical dark energy has a negligible impact on the GW spectrum from cosmic strings.

</details>


### [153] [Analytic results for the two-loop renormalization constants of Feynman gauge QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26750)
*Gregory S. Adkins,Elias Mitchell,A. Volkan Turan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了费米子规范量子电动力学（QED）在壳层方案下费曼规范中的两圈质量重整化常数 $Z_m$ 和顶点/波函数重整化常数 $Z_1 = Z_2$，采用维数正规化方法，并给出了其关于 $\epsilon = (4-d)/2$ 的解析展开式，直至 $\mathcal{O}(\epsilon^5)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持未来多圈QED计算，并为QCD多圈计算提供交叉检验基准，需获得高精度、高阶$\epsilon$展开的解析重整化常数。

Method: 使用维数正规化（dimensional regularization）在费曼规范和on-shell重整化方案下，通过直接费曼图计算与代数简化，导出两圈$Z_m$与$Z_1=Z_2$的完整解析表达式及其$\epsilon$展开系数。

Result: 得到了$Z_m$与$Z_1=Z_2$的闭式解析结果；并首次给出其$\epsilon$展开中从$1/\epsilon^2$至$\epsilon^5$共八阶系数的显式解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该结果构成了QED两圈重整化的完备基准，其高阶$\epsilon$项对高精度轻子反常磁矩计算、QCD–QED混合高阶修正及符号计算验证具有关键作用。

Abstract: The two-loop renormalization constants $Z_m$ and $Z_1\!=\!Z_2$ of Feynman gauge quantum electrodynamics (QED) in the on-shell scheme are computed using dimensional regularization. Analytic results are obtained for these constants. In addition, analytic expressions are obtained for their expansion coefficients in the dimensional regularization parameter $\epsilon$ from order $1/\epsilon^2$ through order $\epsilon^5$. The high-order expansion coefficients will be needed for future multiloop calculations in QED and will provide a useful check for multiloop calculations in QCD.

</details>


### [154] [Froggatt-Nielsen mechanism with non-invertible symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26761)
*Gui-Jun Ding,Stephen F. King,Bu-Yao Qu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Froggatt-Nielsen机制嵌入由ℤ₂规范化的ℤ_N或U(1)所导出的不可逆对称性框架中，利用不可逆选择规则约束费米子质量矩阵的纹理零结构，并引入味子场使融合规则确定小参数ε的幂次，从而同时确定纹理零与非零元的阶数；构造了含九个纹理零的夸克模型及最小I型跷跷板中微子模型，成功复现夸克质量与混合角、正则中微子质量序与一个无质量中微子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一解释费米子质量等级结构与纹理零模式，需超越传统可逆全局/局域对称性，探索更普适的对称性框架——不可逆对称性在近年被发现可自然编码非平凡耦合抑制结构。

Method: 基于ℤ₂ gauging of ℤ_N或U(1)构造非可逆对称性；推导其对应的fusion rules与non-invertible selection rules；引入带特定非可逆荷的flavon场S，使其真空期望值⟨S⟩生成有效小参数ε=⟨S⟩/Λ；通过非可逆算符融合确定Yukawa项允许的ε幂次；构建具体夸克-轻子模型并进行解析估计与数值拟合。

Result: 1) 不可逆选择规则可强制texture zeros，但不能确定非零元大小；2) 引入flavon后，fusion rules固定ε的幂次，从而同时确定texture-zero pattern与各Yukawa矩阵元的阶数（O(1)精度）；3) 夸克模型含9个texture zeros，兼容所有CKM参数与质量比；4) 轻子模型实现minimal type-I seesaw，给出正常等级（normal ordering）、m_ν₁=0，并满足PMNS约束。

Conclusion: 不可逆对称性为Froggatt-Nielsen机制提供了更基本的对称性起源，能统一解释费米子质量层级、混合角及texture-zero结构，是连接量子引力中对称性新范式与粒子物理唯象的重要桥梁。

Abstract: We formulate the Froggatt-Nielsen mechanism in the framework of non-invertible symmetries obtained by $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauging of $\mathbb{Z}_N$ or $U(1)$. While the non-invertible selection rules can constrain the texture-zero structure of fermion mass matrices, they do not by themselves determine the magnitudes of the allowed couplings. Introducing a flavon field allows the fusion rules to fix the leading powers of the small parameter $\varepsilon=\langle S\rangle/\Lambda$, thereby determining both the texture zeros and the orders of magnitude of the nonzero entries up to $\mathcal{O}(1)$ coefficients. We have constructed explicit quark and lepton models and study their predictions for the fermion mass and mixing hierarchies through analytical estimates and numerical analysis. In particular, a benchmark model with nine texture zeros in the quark sector can well accommodate the quark masses and mixing parameters with non-Hermitian Yukawa matrices. Its lepton sector admits a minimal type-I seesaw realization with normal neutrino mass ordering and one massless neutrino. These results provide a common symmetry framework for fermion mass hierarchies and texture zeros.
